**本章主线：** 行为反应 → 观察沟通 → 身体与物体操作 → 人际情绪 → 分派执行 → 修复求证 → 社交回忆

**最后尝试回答：** 选一个生活问题，用明确动作说明你怎样观察、沟通、处理和修复。

### 词群 20.01｜日常行为、反应与维持生命 {#group-20-01}

**串联方式：** 从做事、习惯与回应进入承受、收养、滋养、支持及呼吸，再到复苏和生存；mow 接日常劳动。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-001">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-001" class="vocabulary-word-title">act<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-001" aria-label="定位 act">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-001" data-mark-label="act" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="act：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="act：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=act&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 act" title="在有道查询 act">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-001</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.行动；n.行为</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">One good deed can inspire others to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=act&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>act</strong></a> kindly, behave responsibly and accustom themselves to helping neighbours.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一件善事能鼓励他人友善行动、举止负责，并养成帮助邻居的习惯。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We can accustom children to thoughtful conduct by showing how to behave, when to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=act&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>act</strong></a> and why a good deed matters.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们能通过示范如何举止、何时行动及善行的意义，让孩子习惯体贴待人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=act&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>act</strong></a> with care and behave consistently, I try to accustom myself to doing one helpful deed each day.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为谨慎行动并保持一贯举止，我尝试让自己习惯每天做一件助人的事。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">act responsibly</span> · 负责地行动</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conduct&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">conduct</a></span> · 行为</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=kindness&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">kindness</a></span> · 善意</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-002">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-002" class="vocabulary-word-title">behave<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-002" aria-label="定位 behave">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-002" data-mark-label="behave" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="behave：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="behave：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=behave&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 behave" title="在有道查询 behave">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-002</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.表现；(机器等)运转</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">One good deed can inspire others to act kindly, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=behave&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>behave</strong></a> responsibly and accustom themselves to helping neighbours.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一件善事能鼓励他人友善行动、举止负责，并养成帮助邻居的习惯。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We can accustom children to thoughtful conduct by showing how to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=behave&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>behave</strong></a>, when to act and why a good deed matters.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们能通过示范如何举止、何时行动及善行的意义，让孩子习惯体贴待人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To act with care and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=behave&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>behave</strong></a> consistently, I try to accustom myself to doing one helpful deed each day.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为谨慎行动并保持一贯举止，我尝试让自己习惯每天做一件助人的事。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">behave politely</span> · 举止礼貌</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conduct&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">conduct</a></span> · 行为</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=kindness&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">kindness</a></span> · 善意</li></ul>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">One good deed can inspire others to act kindly, behave responsibly and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accustom&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>accustom</strong></a> themselves to helping neighbours.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一件善事能鼓励他人友善行动、举止负责，并养成帮助邻居的习惯。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-005">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-005" class="vocabulary-word-title">react<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-005" aria-label="定位 react">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-005" data-mark-label="react" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="react：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="react：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=react&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 react" title="在有道查询 react">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-005</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.作出反应，反应</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Good leaders reflect before they <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=react&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>react</strong></a> and respond calmly to criticism.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">优秀领导者在反应前思考，并冷静回应批评。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We should reflect on how people <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=react&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>react</strong></a> to change and respond to their concerns.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们应思考人们如何对变化作反应，并回应其关切。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=react&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>react</strong></a> in anger, take time to reflect and then respond constructively.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要愤怒地反应，先花时间思考，再作出建设性回应。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">react calmly</span> · 冷静反应</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reflection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reflection</a></span> · 反思</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=feedback&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">feedback</a></span> · 反馈</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-006">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-006" class="vocabulary-word-title">respond<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-006" aria-label="定位 respond">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-006" data-mark-label="respond" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="respond：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="respond：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=respond&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 respond" title="在有道查询 respond">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-006</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.回答；响应，作出反应</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Good leaders reflect before they react and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=respond&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>respond</strong></a> calmly to criticism.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">优秀领导者在反应前思考，并冷静回应批评。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We should reflect on how people react to change and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=respond&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>respond</strong></a> to their concerns.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们应思考人们如何对变化作反应，并回应其关切。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than react in anger, take time to reflect and then <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=respond&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>respond</strong></a> constructively.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要愤怒地反应，先花时间思考，再作出建设性回应。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">respond to criticism</span> · 回应批评</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reflection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reflection</a></span> · 反思</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=feedback&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">feedback</a></span> · 反馈</li></ul>
</div>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-007">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-007" class="vocabulary-word-title">reflect<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-007" aria-label="定位 reflect">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-007" data-mark-label="reflect" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="reflect：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="reflect：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reflect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 reflect" title="在有道查询 reflect">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-007</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.反映；反射</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Good leaders <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reflect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reflect</strong></a> before they react and respond calmly to criticism.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">优秀领导者在反应前思考，并冷静回应批评。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We should <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reflect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reflect</strong></a> on how people react to change and respond to their concerns.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们应思考人们如何对变化作反应，并回应其关切。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than react in anger, take time to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reflect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reflect</strong></a> and then respond constructively.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要愤怒地反应，先花时间思考，再作出建设性回应。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">reflect on experience</span> · 反思经历</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reflection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reflection</a></span> · 反思</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=feedback&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">feedback</a></span> · 反馈</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-008">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-008" class="vocabulary-word-title">bear<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-008" aria-label="定位 bear">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-008" data-mark-label="bear" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="bear：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="bear：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 bear" title="在有道查询 bear">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-008</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 承受；承担；忍受；生育；n. 熊（其他语境）</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Residents will support a plan to adopt greener methods, nourish the soil and mow less often if the council can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bear</strong></a> the cost.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果市政机构能承担费用，居民会支持采用更环保方法、滋养土壤并减少割草的计划。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Volunteers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bear</strong></a> the workload, support the garden and adopt techniques that nourish plants without needing to mow every week.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">志愿者承担工作、支持花园，并采用能滋养植物而不必每周割草的技术。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To support wildlife, we could adopt a policy to nourish native plants and mow only paths, though gardeners must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bear</strong></a> the extra planning work.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为支持野生动物，我们可采用滋养本地植物、只修剪小径的政策，但园丁须承担额外规划工作。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">bear the cost</span> · 承担费用</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sustainable%20gardening&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">sustainable gardening</a></span> · 可持续园艺</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=maintenance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">maintenance</a></span> · 维护</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Volunteers bear the workload, support the garden and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=adopt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>adopt</strong></a> techniques that nourish plants without needing to mow every week.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">志愿者承担工作、支持花园，并采用能滋养植物而不必每周割草的技术。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-013">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-013" class="vocabulary-word-title">exhale<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-013" aria-label="定位 exhale">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-013" data-mark-label="exhale" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="exhale：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="exhale：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exhale&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 exhale" title="在有道查询 exhale">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-013</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.呼气，呼出</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After a slow intake of air, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exhale&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>exhale</strong></a> gently.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">缓慢吸气后，轻轻呼气。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The coach watched each intake of breath and reminded swimmers to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exhale&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>exhale</strong></a> underwater.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练观察每次吸气，并提醒游泳者在水下呼气。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The diagram shows air intake before the lungs release air as we <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exhale&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>exhale</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">图示展示了吸入空气，再由肺部在呼气时排出空气的过程。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">exhale slowly</span> · 缓慢呼气</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-014">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After a slow <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>intake</strong></a> of air, exhale gently.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">缓慢吸气后，轻轻呼气。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The coach watched each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>intake</strong></a> of breath and reminded swimmers to exhale underwater.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练观察每次吸气，并提醒游泳者在水下呼气。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The diagram shows air <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>intake</strong></a> before the lungs release air as we exhale.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">图示展示了吸入空气，再由肺部在呼气时排出空气的过程。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">New funding could <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=revive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>revive</strong></a> the theatre and help small arts groups survive.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">新资金可以振兴剧院，并帮助小型艺术团体生存。</p></li>

### 词群 20.02｜看的方式与思考 {#group-20-02}

**串联方式：** 区分怒视、一瞥、偷看、凝视和费力看，再由观察进入沉思。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-017">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-017" class="vocabulary-word-title">glare<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-017" aria-label="定位 glare">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-017" data-mark-label="glare" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="glare：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="glare：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=glare&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 glare" title="在有道查询 glare">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-017</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.怒目而视；发出强光；n.刺眼的光；怒视</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A quick glance let me glimpse the actor's <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=glare&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>glare</strong></a> before he began to peep through the curtain.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">匆匆一眼让我瞥见演员怒视的表情，随后他开始从幕布缝隙偷看。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not peep through the window or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=glare&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>glare</strong></a> at residents; one glance is enough to glimpse the garden from the street.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要从窗户偷看或怒视居民；从街上看一眼就足以瞥见花园。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In the scene, the child takes a glance at the door, tries to peep outside and catches a glimpse of her father's <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=glare&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>glare</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">场景中，孩子看了一眼门、试着偷看外面，并瞥见父亲怒视的目光。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">glare at someone</span> · 怒视某人</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=facial%20expression&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">facial expression</a></span> · 面部表情</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=privacy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">privacy</a></span> · 隐私</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-018">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not peep through the window or glare at residents; one glance is enough to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=glimpse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>glimpse</strong></a> the garden from the street.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要从窗户偷看或怒视居民；从街上看一眼就足以瞥见花园。</p></li>

<summary><h4 id="word-20-021" class="vocabulary-word-title">gaze<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-021" aria-label="定位 gaze">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-021" data-mark-label="gaze" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="gaze：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="gaze：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gaze&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 gaze" title="在有道查询 gaze">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-021</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.凝视，注视</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Visitors <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gaze&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gaze</strong></a> at the sky, peer through telescopes, stare at distant planets and contemplate the universe.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">访客凝望天空、透过望远镜细看、盯着遥远行星，并思考宇宙。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than stare at a phone, I prefer to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gaze&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gaze</strong></a> at the sea, peer into rock pools and contemplate nature.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我宁愿凝望大海、仔细看潮池并思考自然，也不愿盯着手机。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The artist asks viewers to peer at details, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gaze&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gaze</strong></a> at the whole painting, stare briefly at its central figure and contemplate its message.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">艺术家请观众仔细看细节、凝视整幅画、短暂盯住中心人物，并思考其寓意。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">gaze at the stars</span> · 凝视星空</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=observation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">observation</a></span> · 观察</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attention&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">attention</a></span> · 注意</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stare&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stare</strong></a> at a phone, I prefer to gaze at the sea, peer into rock pools and contemplate nature.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我宁愿凝望大海、仔细看潮池并思考自然，也不愿盯着手机。</p></li>

### 词群 20.03｜承诺、通知与争论 {#group-20-03}

**串联方式：** 用誓言和保证建立承诺，再回复、通知、说明与提及，最后辨争吵和争议。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-025">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-025" class="vocabulary-word-title">vow<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-025" aria-label="定位 vow">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-025" data-mark-label="vow" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="vow：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="vow：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 vow" title="在有道查询 vow">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-025</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.誓约；v.发誓</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The official took an oath, made a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vow</strong></a> to serve honestly and renewed a pledge to protect rights.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">官员宣了誓，许诺诚实服务，并重申保护权利的承诺。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A wedding <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vow</strong></a> differs from a public oath, but both can express a solemn pledge.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">婚姻誓言不同于公开宣誓，但两者都可表达庄严承诺。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The memoir recalls an oath of office, a personal <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vow</strong></a> and a pledge that proved difficult to keep.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">回忆录追忆就职誓言、个人誓愿及一项难以兑现的承诺。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">make a vow</span> · 许愿</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commitment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">commitment</a></span> · 承诺</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=solemnity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">solemnity</a></span> · 庄严</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-026">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-026" class="vocabulary-word-title">oath<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-026" aria-label="定位 oath">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-026" data-mark-label="oath" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="oath：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="oath：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oath&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 oath" title="在有道查询 oath">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-026</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.誓言；宣誓</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The official took an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oath&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>oath</strong></a>, made a vow to serve honestly and renewed a pledge to protect rights.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">官员宣了誓，许诺诚实服务，并重申保护权利的承诺。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A wedding vow differs from a public <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oath&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>oath</strong></a>, but both can express a solemn pledge.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">婚姻誓言不同于公开宣誓，但两者都可表达庄严承诺。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The memoir recalls an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=oath&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>oath</strong></a> of office, a personal vow and a pledge that proved difficult to keep.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">回忆录追忆就职誓言、个人誓愿及一项难以兑现的承诺。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">take an oath</span> · 宣誓</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commitment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">commitment</a></span> · 承诺</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=solemnity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">solemnity</a></span> · 庄严</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-027">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-027" class="vocabulary-word-title">pledge<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-027" aria-label="定位 pledge">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-027" data-mark-label="pledge" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="pledge：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="pledge：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pledge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 pledge" title="在有道查询 pledge">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-027</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 保证；n.保证，誓言</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The official took an oath, made a vow to serve honestly and renewed a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pledge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pledge</strong></a> to protect rights.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">官员宣了誓，许诺诚实服务，并重申保护权利的承诺。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A wedding vow differs from a public oath, but both can express a solemn <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pledge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pledge</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">婚姻誓言不同于公开宣誓，但两者都可表达庄严承诺。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The memoir recalls an oath of office, a personal vow and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pledge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pledge</strong></a> that proved difficult to keep.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">回忆录追忆就职誓言、个人誓愿及一项难以兑现的承诺。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a public pledge</span> · 公开承诺</p>

</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-028">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-028" class="vocabulary-word-title">whistle<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-028" aria-label="定位 whistle">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-028" data-mark-label="whistle" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="whistle：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="whistle：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=whistle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 whistle" title="在有道查询 whistle">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-028</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.口哨；哨子；汽笛声；v.吹口哨；吹哨子</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The coach used a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=whistle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>whistle</strong></a> to notify players that practice was over and waited for their reply.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练吹哨通知球员训练结束，并等待回应。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The station guard will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=whistle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>whistle</strong></a> to notify staff, who must send a reply by radio.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">车站警卫将吹哨通知工作人员，对方必须用无线电回复。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=whistle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>whistle</strong></a> can notify rescuers of your position when you cannot shout a reply.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">当你无法大声回应时，哨声能通知救援人员你的位置。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">blow a whistle</span> · 吹哨</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=signal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">signal</a></span> · 信号</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=communication&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">communication</a></span> · 沟通</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-029">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-029" class="vocabulary-word-title">reply<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-029" aria-label="定位 reply">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-029" data-mark-label="reply" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="reply：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="reply：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reply&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 reply" title="在有道查询 reply">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-029</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.回答，答复；n.回答，答复</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The coach used a whistle to notify players that practice was over and waited for their <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reply&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reply</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练吹哨通知球员训练结束，并等待回应。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The station guard will whistle to notify staff, who must send a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reply&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reply</strong></a> by radio.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">车站警卫将吹哨通知工作人员，对方必须用无线电回复。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A whistle can notify rescuers of your position when you cannot shout a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reply&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reply</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">当你无法大声回应时，哨声能通知救援人员你的位置。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">give a reply</span> · 回复</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=signal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">signal</a></span> · 信号</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=communication&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">communication</a></span> · 沟通</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-030">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-030" class="vocabulary-word-title">notify<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-030" aria-label="定位 notify">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-030" data-mark-label="notify" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="notify：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="notify：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=notify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 notify" title="在有道查询 notify">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-030</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.通知，告知</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The coach used a whistle to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=notify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>notify</strong></a> players that practice was over and waited for their reply.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练吹哨通知球员训练结束，并等待回应。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The station guard will whistle to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=notify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>notify</strong></a> staff, who must send a reply by radio.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">车站警卫将吹哨通知工作人员，对方必须用无线电回复。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A whistle can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=notify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>notify</strong></a> rescuers of your position when you cannot shout a reply.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">当你无法大声回应时，哨声能通知救援人员你的位置。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">notify the team</span> · 通知团队</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=signal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">signal</a></span> · 信号</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=communication&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">communication</a></span> · 沟通</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-031">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-031" class="vocabulary-word-title">assert<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-031" aria-label="定位 assert">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-031" data-mark-label="assert" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="assert：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="assert：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assert&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 assert" title="在有道查询 assert">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-031</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v断言，坚称；坚持(自己)的主张</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Please explain the facts, mention your concern and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assert&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>assert</strong></a> your right to a fair review.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">请解释事实、提及关切，并主张获得公平审查的权利。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Witnesses should explain what happened, mention relevant details and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assert&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>assert</strong></a> only what they know.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">证人应解释发生的事、提及相关细节，并只主张自己知道的内容。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assert&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>assert</strong></a> our position calmly, explain our reasoning and mention evidence that supports it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们可以冷静主张立场、解释推理，并提及支持它的证据。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">assert a right</span> · 主张权利</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reasoning&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reasoning</a></span> · 推理</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=evidence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">evidence</a></span> · 证据</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-032">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-032" class="vocabulary-word-title">explain<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-032" aria-label="定位 explain">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-032" data-mark-label="explain" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="explain：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="explain：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=explain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 explain" title="在有道查询 explain">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-032</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.说明，解释</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Please <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=explain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>explain</strong></a> the facts, mention your concern and assert your right to a fair review.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">请解释事实、提及关切，并主张获得公平审查的权利。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Witnesses should <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=explain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>explain</strong></a> what happened, mention relevant details and assert only what they know.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">证人应解释发生的事、提及相关细节，并只主张自己知道的内容。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We can assert our position calmly, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=explain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>explain</strong></a> our reasoning and mention evidence that supports it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们可以冷静主张立场、解释推理，并提及支持它的证据。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">explain a reason</span> · 解释原因</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reasoning&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reasoning</a></span> · 推理</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=evidence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">evidence</a></span> · 证据</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-033">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-033" class="vocabulary-word-title">quarrel<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-033" aria-label="定位 quarrel">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-033" data-mark-label="quarrel" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="quarrel：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="quarrel：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=quarrel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 quarrel" title="在有道查询 quarrel">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-033</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.吵架，争论；n.吵架，争论</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A reasoned argument can resolve a dispute without a bitter <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=quarrel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>quarrel</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有理有据的论证可以解决争议，而无须激烈争吵。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The dispute began as a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=quarrel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>quarrel</strong></a> over rent, but the landlord's argument failed to convince the tenant.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">争议起初是关于租金的争吵，但房东的论点未能说服租客。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=quarrel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>quarrel</strong></a>, each side should present an argument that addresses the actual dispute.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">双方不应争吵，而应提出切中实际争议的论点。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">quarrel over money</span> · 为钱争吵</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mediation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">mediation</a></span> · 调解</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dialogue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dialogue</a></span> · 对话</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-034">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-034" class="vocabulary-word-title">dispute<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-034" aria-label="定位 dispute">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-034" data-mark-label="dispute" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="dispute：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="dispute：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dispute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 dispute" title="在有道查询 dispute">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-034</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.争论，辩论；n.争论，辩论</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A reasoned argument can resolve a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dispute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dispute</strong></a> without a bitter quarrel.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有理有据的论证可以解决争议，而无须激烈争吵。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dispute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dispute</strong></a> began as a quarrel over rent, but the landlord's argument failed to convince the tenant.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">争议起初是关于租金的争吵，但房东的论点未能说服租客。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than quarrel, each side should present an argument that addresses the actual <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dispute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dispute</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">双方不应争吵，而应提出切中实际争议的论点。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">resolve a dispute</span> · 解决争议</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mediation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">mediation</a></span> · 调解</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dialogue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dialogue</a></span> · 对话</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-035">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-035" class="vocabulary-word-title">argument<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-035" aria-label="定位 argument">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-035" data-mark-label="argument" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="argument：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="argument：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=argument&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 argument" title="在有道查询 argument">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-035</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.辩论，争论；论点，论据</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A reasoned <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=argument&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>argument</strong></a> can resolve a dispute without a bitter quarrel.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有理有据的论证可以解决争议，而无须激烈争吵。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The dispute began as a quarrel over rent, but the landlord's <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=argument&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>argument</strong></a> failed to convince the tenant.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">争议起初是关于租金的争吵，但房东的论点未能说服租客。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than quarrel, each side should present an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=argument&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>argument</strong></a> that addresses the actual dispute.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">双方不应争吵，而应提出切中实际争议的论点。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a persuasive argument</span> · 有说服力的论证</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mediation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">mediation</a></span> · 调解</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dialogue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dialogue</a></span> · 对话</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-036">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-036" class="vocabulary-word-title">mention<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-036" aria-label="定位 mention">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-036" data-mark-label="mention" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="mention：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="mention：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mention&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 mention" title="在有道查询 mention">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-036</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.提及，说起；n.提及，说起</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Please explain the facts, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mention&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mention</strong></a> your concern and assert your right to a fair review.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">请解释事实、提及关切，并主张获得公平审查的权利。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Witnesses should explain what happened, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mention&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mention</strong></a> relevant details and assert only what they know.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">证人应解释发生的事、提及相关细节，并只主张自己知道的内容。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We can assert our position calmly, explain our reasoning and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mention&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mention</strong></a> evidence that supports it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们可以冷静主张立场、解释推理，并提及支持它的证据。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">mention a problem</span> · 提及问题</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reasoning&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reasoning</a></span> · 推理</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=evidence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">evidence</a></span> · 证据</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 20.04｜催促与带情绪的言语 {#group-20-04}

**串联方式：** 从匆忙、催促进入责骂、诅咒、挑衅、说教、自夸和嘲弄。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-037">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-037" class="vocabulary-word-title">hurry<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-037" aria-label="定位 hurry">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-037" data-mark-label="hurry" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="hurry：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="hurry：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hurry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 hurry" title="在有道查询 hurry">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-037</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.赶紧；催促；n.匆忙</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Doctors urge caution because a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hurry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hurry</strong></a> to approve treatment can hasten a poor decision.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">医生敦促谨慎，因为急于批准治疗可能促成草率决定。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hurry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hurry</strong></a> the team or urge them to hasten repairs before safety checks.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">安全检查前，不要催团队或敦促他们加快维修。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The managers urge us to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hurry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hurry</strong></a>, but extra pressure may not hasten careful work.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">经理催促我们赶快，但额外压力未必能加快细致工作。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">in a hurry</span> · 匆忙地</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=urgency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">urgency</a></span> · 紧迫</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=patience&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">patience</a></span> · 耐心</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-038">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-038" class="vocabulary-word-title">hasten<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-038" aria-label="定位 hasten">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-038" data-mark-label="hasten" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="hasten：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="hasten：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hasten&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 hasten" title="在有道查询 hasten">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-038</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 赶紧；促进</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Doctors urge caution because a hurry to approve treatment can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hasten&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hasten</strong></a> a poor decision.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">医生敦促谨慎，因为急于批准治疗可能促成草率决定。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not hurry the team or urge them to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hasten&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hasten</strong></a> repairs before safety checks.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">安全检查前，不要催团队或敦促他们加快维修。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The managers urge us to hurry, but extra pressure may not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hasten&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hasten</strong></a> careful work.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">经理催促我们赶快，但额外压力未必能加快细致工作。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">hasten a decision</span> · 加快决定</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=urgency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">urgency</a></span> · 紧迫</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=patience&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">patience</a></span> · 耐心</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-039">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-039" class="vocabulary-word-title">urge<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-039" aria-label="定位 urge">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-039" data-mark-label="urge" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="urge：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="urge：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=urge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 urge" title="在有道查询 urge">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-039</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.催促，敦促n冲动</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Doctors <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=urge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>urge</strong></a> caution because a hurry to approve treatment can hasten a poor decision.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">医生敦促谨慎，因为急于批准治疗可能促成草率决定。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not hurry the team or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=urge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>urge</strong></a> them to hasten repairs before safety checks.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">安全检查前，不要催团队或敦促他们加快维修。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The managers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=urge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>urge</strong></a> us to hurry, but extra pressure may not hasten careful work.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">经理催促我们赶快，但额外压力未必能加快细致工作。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">urge caution</span> · 敦促谨慎</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=urgency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">urgency</a></span> · 紧迫</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=patience&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">patience</a></span> · 耐心</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-040">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-040" class="vocabulary-word-title">scold<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-040" aria-label="定位 scold">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-040" data-mark-label="scold" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="scold：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="scold：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=scold&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 scold" title="在有道查询 scold">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-040</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.责骂，斥责</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=scold&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>scold</strong></a> a child, curse or swear can provoke fear rather than understanding.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">责骂孩子、咒骂或说脏话可能引起恐惧，而非理解。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The coach refused to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=scold&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>scold</strong></a> players, swear at officials or curse a result that might provoke anger.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练拒绝责骂球员、对裁判说脏话，或咒骂可能令人愤怒的结果。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Adults should not curse or swear when they <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=scold&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>scold</strong></a> children, since harsh language can provoke distress.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">大人责备孩子时不该咒骂或说脏话，因为严厉语言可能引发痛苦。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">scold a child</span> · 责骂孩子</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=verbal%20abuse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">verbal abuse</a></span> · 言语虐待</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=self-control&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">self-control</a></span> · 自制</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-041">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-041" class="vocabulary-word-title">curse<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-041" aria-label="定位 curse">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-041" data-mark-label="curse" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="curse：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="curse：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=curse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 curse" title="在有道查询 curse">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-041</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.诅咒；咒骂n咒语：咒骂话</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To scold a child, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=curse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>curse</strong></a> or swear can provoke fear rather than understanding.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">责骂孩子、咒骂或说脏话可能引起恐惧，而非理解。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The coach refused to scold players, swear at officials or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=curse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>curse</strong></a> a result that might provoke anger.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练拒绝责骂球员、对裁判说脏话，或咒骂可能令人愤怒的结果。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Adults should not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=curse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>curse</strong></a> or swear when they scold children, since harsh language can provoke distress.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">大人责备孩子时不该咒骂或说脏话，因为严厉语言可能引发痛苦。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">curse in anger</span> · 愤怒地咒骂</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=verbal%20abuse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">verbal abuse</a></span> · 言语虐待</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=self-control&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">self-control</a></span> · 自制</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-042">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-042" class="vocabulary-word-title">swear<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-042" aria-label="定位 swear">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-042" data-mark-label="swear" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="swear：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="swear：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=swear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 swear" title="在有道查询 swear">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-042</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v诅咒，咒骂；发苔，保证</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To scold a child, curse or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=swear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>swear</strong></a> can provoke fear rather than understanding.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">责骂孩子、咒骂或说脏话可能引起恐惧，而非理解。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The coach refused to scold players, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=swear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>swear</strong></a> at officials or curse a result that might provoke anger.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练拒绝责骂球员、对裁判说脏话，或咒骂可能令人愤怒的结果。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Adults should not curse or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=swear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>swear</strong></a> when they scold children, since harsh language can provoke distress.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">大人责备孩子时不该咒骂或说脏话，因为严厉语言可能引发痛苦。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">swear loudly</span> · 大声说脏话</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=verbal%20abuse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">verbal abuse</a></span> · 言语虐待</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=self-control&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">self-control</a></span> · 自制</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-043">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-043" class="vocabulary-word-title">provoke<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-043" aria-label="定位 provoke">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-043" data-mark-label="provoke" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="provoke：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="provoke：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=provoke&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 provoke" title="在有道查询 provoke">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-043</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.激怒，挑拨；引起</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To scold a child, curse or swear can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=provoke&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>provoke</strong></a> fear rather than understanding.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">责骂孩子、咒骂或说脏话可能引起恐惧，而非理解。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The coach refused to scold players, swear at officials or curse a result that might <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=provoke&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>provoke</strong></a> anger.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练拒绝责骂球员、对裁判说脏话，或咒骂可能令人愤怒的结果。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Adults should not curse or swear when they scold children, since harsh language can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=provoke&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>provoke</strong></a> distress.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">大人责备孩子时不该咒骂或说脏话，因为严厉语言可能引发痛苦。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">provoke anger</span> · 激怒</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=verbal%20abuse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">verbal abuse</a></span> · 言语虐待</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=self-control&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">self-control</a></span> · 自制</li></ul>
</div>
