<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-044">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-044" class="vocabulary-word-title">preach<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-044" aria-label="定位 preach">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-044" data-mark-label="preach" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="preach：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="preach：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=preach&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 preach" title="在有道查询 preach">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-044</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.宣扬</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">People who <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=preach&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>preach</strong></a> tolerance should not boast of their status, tease strangers or mock an accent.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">宣扬宽容的人不该夸耀地位、戏弄陌生人或嘲笑口音。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Teachers can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=preach&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>preach</strong></a> respect, but they must also avoid the urge to boast, tease or mock.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">老师可以宣讲尊重，但也必须避免夸耀、戏弄或嘲笑的冲动。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The speaker began to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=preach&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>preach</strong></a> kindness, then admitted he used to boast, tease classmates and mock their mistakes.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">演讲者开始宣扬善意，随后承认自己过去常夸耀、戏弄同学并嘲笑他们的错误。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">preach tolerance</span> · 宣扬宽容</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=respect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">respect</a></span> · 尊重</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=humility&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">humility</a></span> · 谦逊</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-045">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-045" class="vocabulary-word-title">boast<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-045" aria-label="定位 boast">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-045" data-mark-label="boast" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="boast：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="boast：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=boast&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 boast" title="在有道查询 boast">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-045</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.自夸，吹嘘；n.自夸，吹嘘</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">People who preach tolerance should not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=boast&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>boast</strong></a> of their status, tease strangers or mock an accent.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">宣扬宽容的人不该夸耀地位、戏弄陌生人或嘲笑口音。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Teachers can preach respect, but they must also avoid the urge to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=boast&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>boast</strong></a>, tease or mock.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">老师可以宣讲尊重，但也必须避免夸耀、戏弄或嘲笑的冲动。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The speaker began to preach kindness, then admitted he used to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=boast&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>boast</strong></a>, tease classmates and mock their mistakes.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">演讲者开始宣扬善意，随后承认自己过去常夸耀、戏弄同学并嘲笑他们的错误。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">boast about success</span> · 夸耀成功</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=respect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">respect</a></span> · 尊重</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=humility&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">humility</a></span> · 谦逊</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">People who preach tolerance should not boast of their status, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tease&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tease</strong></a> strangers or mock an accent.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">宣扬宽容的人不该夸耀地位、戏弄陌生人或嘲笑口音。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Teachers can preach respect, but they must also avoid the urge to boast, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tease&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tease</strong></a> or mock.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">老师可以宣讲尊重，但也必须避免夸耀、戏弄或嘲笑的冲动。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The speaker began to preach kindness, then admitted he used to boast, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tease&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tease</strong></a> classmates and mock their mistakes.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">演讲者开始宣扬善意，随后承认自己过去常夸耀、戏弄同学并嘲笑他们的错误。</p></li>

### 词群 20.05｜接触、身体姿态与抓握 {#group-20-05}

**串联方式：** 从触碰、拥抱和拍手进入折叠、踉跄、下跪，再辨接住、抢夺、抓牢和跟随。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-048">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-048" class="vocabulary-word-title">touch<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-048" aria-label="定位 touch">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-048" data-mark-label="touch" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="touch：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="touch：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=touch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 touch" title="在有道查询 touch">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-048</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.触摸；触动；n.触觉；接触</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A gentle <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=touch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>touch</strong></a>, a hug or a tap on the shoulder can reassure a friend.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">轻轻触碰、拥抱或拍肩，能让朋友安心。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Ask before you <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=touch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>touch</strong></a> someone, give a hug or tap their arm.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">触碰别人、拥抱或轻拍其手臂前，应先询问。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The child welcomed a hug and a gentle <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=touch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>touch</strong></a> but disliked a sudden tap on the back.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">孩子喜欢拥抱和温柔触碰，却不喜欢背上突然被拍一下。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a gentle touch</span> · 轻柔触碰</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">consent</a></span> · 同意</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reassurance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reassurance</a></span> · 安慰</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-049">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-049" class="vocabulary-word-title">hug<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-049" aria-label="定位 hug">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-049" data-mark-label="hug" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="hug：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="hug：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hug&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 hug" title="在有道查询 hug">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-049</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.拥抱；n.拥抱</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A gentle touch, a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hug&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hug</strong></a> or a tap on the shoulder can reassure a friend.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">轻轻触碰、拥抱或拍肩，能让朋友安心。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Ask before you touch someone, give a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hug&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hug</strong></a> or tap their arm.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">触碰别人、拥抱或轻拍其手臂前，应先询问。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The child welcomed a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hug&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hug</strong></a> and a gentle touch but disliked a sudden tap on the back.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">孩子喜欢拥抱和温柔触碰，却不喜欢背上突然被拍一下。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">give a hug</span> · 拥抱</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">consent</a></span> · 同意</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reassurance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reassurance</a></span> · 安慰</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-050">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-050" class="vocabulary-word-title">tap<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-050" aria-label="定位 tap">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-050" data-mark-label="tap" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="tap：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="tap：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 tap" title="在有道查询 tap">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-050</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.轻拍，轻叩；开发</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A gentle touch, a hug or a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tap</strong></a> on the shoulder can reassure a friend.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">轻轻触碰、拥抱或拍肩，能让朋友安心。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Ask before you touch someone, give a hug or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tap</strong></a> their arm.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">触碰别人、拥抱或轻拍其手臂前，应先询问。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The child welcomed a hug and a gentle touch but disliked a sudden <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tap</strong></a> on the back.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">孩子喜欢拥抱和温柔触碰，却不喜欢背上突然被拍一下。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">tap a shoulder</span> · 轻拍肩膀</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">consent</a></span> · 同意</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reassurance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reassurance</a></span> · 安慰</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-051">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-051" class="vocabulary-word-title">clap<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-051" aria-label="定位 clap">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-051" data-mark-label="clap" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="clap：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="clap：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=clap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 clap" title="在有道查询 clap">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-051</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.拍手；轻拍；n.鼓掌</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The audience began to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=clap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>clap</strong></a> and applaud the dancers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">观众开始拍手，为舞者鼓掌。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Parents <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=clap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>clap</strong></a> when children succeed, but should also applaud their effort.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">父母在孩子成功时拍手，也应为其努力喝彩。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We can applaud a speaker politely without having to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=clap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>clap</strong></a> throughout the speech.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们可以礼貌地为演讲者鼓掌，不必在整场演讲中不断拍手。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">clap your hands</span> · 拍手</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=appreciation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">appreciation</a></span> · 赞赏</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=audience&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">audience</a></span> · 观众</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-052">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-052" class="vocabulary-word-title">applaud<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-052" aria-label="定位 applaud">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-052" data-mark-label="applaud" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="applaud：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="applaud：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=applaud&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 applaud" title="在有道查询 applaud">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-052</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.鼓掌；称赞，赞许</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The audience began to clap and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=applaud&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>applaud</strong></a> the dancers.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">观众开始拍手，为舞者鼓掌。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Parents clap when children succeed, but should also <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=applaud&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>applaud</strong></a> their effort.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">父母在孩子成功时拍手，也应为其努力喝彩。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=applaud&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>applaud</strong></a> a speaker politely without having to clap throughout the speech.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们可以礼貌地为演讲者鼓掌，不必在整场演讲中不断拍手。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">applaud a performance</span> · 为表演鼓掌</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=appreciation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">appreciation</a></span> · 赞赏</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=audience&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">audience</a></span> · 观众</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-053">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-053" class="vocabulary-word-title">fold<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-053" aria-label="定位 fold">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-053" data-mark-label="fold" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="fold：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="fold：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fold&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 fold" title="在有道查询 fold">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-053</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.折叠，对折</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The camper had to kneel beside the lake to reel in the line and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fold&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fold</strong></a> the wet blanket.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">露营者不得不跪在湖边收钓线，并折叠湿毯子。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Kneel on the mat, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fold&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fold</strong></a> the cloth and use it to hold the fishing reel.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">跪在垫上，折好布，用它握住渔线轮。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We watched the fisherman kneel, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fold&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fold</strong></a> his jacket and reel in the empty line.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们看着渔民跪下、叠好外套，再收回空钓线。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">fold a blanket</span> · 折叠毯子</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fishing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">fishing</a></span> · 钓鱼</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=camping&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">camping</a></span> · 露营</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-054">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-054" class="vocabulary-word-title">reel<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-054" aria-label="定位 reel">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-054" data-mark-label="reel" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="reel：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="reel：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 reel" title="在有道查询 reel">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-054</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 眩晕，混乱</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The camper had to kneel beside the lake to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reel</strong></a> in the line and fold the wet blanket.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">露营者不得不跪在湖边收钓线，并折叠湿毯子。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Kneel on the mat, fold the cloth and use it to hold the fishing <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reel</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">跪在垫上，折好布，用它握住渔线轮。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We watched the fisherman kneel, fold his jacket and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reel</strong></a> in the empty line.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们看着渔民跪下、叠好外套，再收回空钓线。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">reel in a line</span> · 收卷钓线</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fishing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">fishing</a></span> · 钓鱼</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=camping&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">camping</a></span> · 露营</li></ul>
</div>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-055">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-055" class="vocabulary-word-title">kneel<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-055" aria-label="定位 kneel">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-055" data-mark-label="kneel" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="kneel：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="kneel：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=kneel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 kneel" title="在有道查询 kneel">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-055</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.跪，跪下</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The camper had to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=kneel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>kneel</strong></a> beside the lake to reel in the line and fold the wet blanket.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">露营者不得不跪在湖边收钓线，并折叠湿毯子。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=kneel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Kneel</strong></a> on the mat, fold the cloth and use it to hold the fishing reel.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">跪在垫上，折好布，用它握住渔线轮。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We watched the fisherman <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=kneel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>kneel</strong></a>, fold his jacket and reel in the empty line.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们看着渔民跪下、叠好外套，再收回空钓线。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">kneel on the ground</span> · 跪在地上</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-056">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-056" class="vocabulary-word-title">catch<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-056" aria-label="定位 catch">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-056" data-mark-label="catch" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="catch：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="catch：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=catch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 catch" title="在有道查询 catch">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-056</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v捉住；赶上；领会</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The trainer showed how to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=catch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>catch</strong></a> the rope, grab the handle and maintain a grip without trying to snatch or grasp too suddenly.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练示范如何接住绳子、抓住把手并保持握力，而不要突然猛抓。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A secure grip helps rescuers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=catch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>catch</strong></a> a slipping hand, grasp a rail and grab a rope rather than snatch at loose clothing.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">牢固抓握帮助救援者接住滑落的手、握住栏杆并抓住绳子，而非猛扯松衣服。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Children learn to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=catch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>catch</strong></a> a ball, grasp a bat and grab a handle, but must not snatch toys from another child's grip.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">孩子学习接球、握球棒和抓把手，但不能从别的孩子手中抢玩具。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">catch a ball</span> · 接球</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=coordination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">coordination</a></span> · 协调能力</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hand%20strength&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hand strength</a></span> · 手部力量</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-060">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-060" class="vocabulary-word-title">overtake<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-060" aria-label="定位 overtake">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-060" data-mark-label="overtake" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="overtake：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="overtake：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=overtake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 overtake" title="在有道查询 overtake">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-060</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.追上，超过；(情感等)压倒</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Drivers should follow the lane markings and only <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=overtake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>overtake</strong></a> when it is safe.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">司机应沿车道标线行驶，仅在安全时超车。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Follow the lead car, but do not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=overtake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>overtake</strong></a> it on the narrow road.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">跟随前车，但不要在窄路上超车。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A cyclist may follow the bus until there is enough room to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=overtake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>overtake</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">骑车人可先跟随公交车，等空间充足再超车。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">overtake a vehicle</span> · 超车</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=road%20safety&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">road safety</a></span> · 道路安全</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=traffic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">traffic</a></span> · 交通</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-061">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-061" class="vocabulary-word-title">follow<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-061" aria-label="定位 follow">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-061" data-mark-label="follow" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="follow：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="follow：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=follow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 follow" title="在有道查询 follow">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-061</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.跟随；是......的必然结果；遵循</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Drivers should <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=follow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>follow</strong></a> the lane markings and only overtake when it is safe.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">司机应沿车道标线行驶，仅在安全时超车。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=follow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Follow</strong></a> the lead car, but do not overtake it on the narrow road.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">跟随前车，但不要在窄路上超车。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A cyclist may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=follow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>follow</strong></a> the bus until there is enough room to overtake.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">骑车人可先跟随公交车，等空间充足再超车。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">follow a route</span> · 沿路线前进</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=road%20safety&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">road safety</a></span> · 道路安全</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=traffic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">traffic</a></span> · 交通</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-062">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A secure <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=grip&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>grip</strong></a> helps rescuers catch a slipping hand, grasp a rail and grab a rope rather than snatch at loose clothing.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">牢固抓握帮助救援者接住滑落的手、握住栏杆并抓住绳子，而非猛扯松衣服。</p></li>

### 词群 20.06｜整理、固定与处理表面 {#group-20-06}

**串联方式：** 由杂乱、扭动和散落进入紧固、松开、击碎、擦拭、刮削、抛光和剥离。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-063">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-063" class="vocabulary-word-title">mess<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-063" aria-label="定位 mess">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-063" data-mark-label="mess" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="mess：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="mess：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mess&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 mess" title="在有道查询 mess">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-063</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If you twist the open bag, seeds may scatter and make a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mess&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mess</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果扭动开口的袋子，种子可能撒落，弄得一团糟。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not scatter the pieces or twist the strings into a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mess&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mess</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要把零件撒得到处都是，或把绳子扭成一团。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The art activity lets children twist paper and scatter glitter, although it creates a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mess&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mess</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">美术活动让孩子扭纸、撒亮粉，尽管会弄乱场地。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">make a mess</span> · 弄乱</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tidiness&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">tidiness</a></span> · 整洁</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=craft%20activity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">craft activity</a></span> · 手工活动</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-064">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-064" class="vocabulary-word-title">twist<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-064" aria-label="定位 twist">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-064" data-mark-label="twist" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="twist：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="twist：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=twist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 twist" title="在有道查询 twist">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-064</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.拧，扭；使缠绕；n.拧，扭动</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If you <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=twist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>twist</strong></a> the open bag, seeds may scatter and make a mess.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果扭动开口的袋子，种子可能撒落，弄得一团糟。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not scatter the pieces or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=twist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>twist</strong></a> the strings into a mess.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要把零件撒得到处都是，或把绳子扭成一团。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The art activity lets children <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=twist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>twist</strong></a> paper and scatter glitter, although it creates a mess.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">美术活动让孩子扭纸、撒亮粉，尽管会弄乱场地。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">twist a wire</span> · 扭动电线</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tidiness&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">tidiness</a></span> · 整洁</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=craft%20activity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">craft activity</a></span> · 手工活动</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-065">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-065" class="vocabulary-word-title">scatter<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-065" aria-label="定位 scatter">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-065" data-mark-label="scatter" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="scatter：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="scatter：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=scatter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 scatter" title="在有道查询 scatter">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-065</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If you twist the open bag, seeds may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=scatter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>scatter</strong></a> and make a mess.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果扭动开口的袋子，种子可能撒落，弄得一团糟。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=scatter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>scatter</strong></a> the pieces or twist the strings into a mess.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要把零件撒得到处都是，或把绳子扭成一团。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The art activity lets children twist paper and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=scatter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>scatter</strong></a> glitter, although it creates a mess.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">美术活动让孩子扭纸、撒亮粉，尽管会弄乱场地。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-066">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-066" class="vocabulary-word-title">fasten<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-066" aria-label="定位 fasten">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-066" data-mark-label="fasten" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="fasten：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="fasten：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fasten&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 fasten" title="在有道查询 fasten">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-066</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.拍牢，系牢，缚紧</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fasten&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Fasten</strong></a> the frame carefully, loosen tight screws and avoid tools that might scratch the glass or smash it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">仔细固定框架，拧松过紧螺丝，并避免使用可能划伤或砸碎玻璃的工具。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If you loosen the hinge before you <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fasten&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fasten</strong></a> the door, it may swing and scratch or smash the mirror.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">若固定门之前先松开铰链，门可能摆动，划伤或撞碎镜子。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Workers must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fasten&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fasten</strong></a> protective covers, loosen fittings gently and avoid actions that scratch surfaces or smash fragile parts.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工人须固定保护罩、轻轻松开配件，并避免划伤表面或打碎易碎零件。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">fasten a belt</span> · 系紧腰带</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=repair&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">repair</a></span> · 维修</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=surface%20protection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">surface protection</a></span> · 表面保护</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-067">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-067" class="vocabulary-word-title">loosen<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-067" aria-label="定位 loosen">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-067" data-mark-label="loosen" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="loosen：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="loosen：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=loosen&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 loosen" title="在有道查询 loosen">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-067</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v解开，松开；放宽(限制等)</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Fasten the frame carefully, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=loosen&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>loosen</strong></a> tight screws and avoid tools that might scratch the glass or smash it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">仔细固定框架，拧松过紧螺丝，并避免使用可能划伤或砸碎玻璃的工具。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If you <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=loosen&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>loosen</strong></a> the hinge before you fasten the door, it may swing and scratch or smash the mirror.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">若固定门之前先松开铰链，门可能摆动，划伤或撞碎镜子。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Workers must fasten protective covers, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=loosen&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>loosen</strong></a> fittings gently and avoid actions that scratch surfaces or smash fragile parts.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工人须固定保护罩、轻轻松开配件，并避免划伤表面或打碎易碎零件。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">loosen a screw</span> · 拧松螺丝</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=repair&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">repair</a></span> · 维修</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=surface%20protection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">surface protection</a></span> · 表面保护</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-068">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-068" class="vocabulary-word-title">smash<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-068" aria-label="定位 smash">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-068" data-mark-label="smash" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="smash：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="smash：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=smash&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 smash" title="在有道查询 smash">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-068</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.打碎；(使)猛烈撞击；(打球时)扣球</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Fasten the frame carefully, loosen tight screws and avoid tools that might scratch the glass or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=smash&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>smash</strong></a> it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">仔细固定框架，拧松过紧螺丝，并避免使用可能划伤或砸碎玻璃的工具。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If you loosen the hinge before you fasten the door, it may swing and scratch or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=smash&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>smash</strong></a> the mirror.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">若固定门之前先松开铰链，门可能摆动，划伤或撞碎镜子。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Workers must fasten protective covers, loosen fittings gently and avoid actions that scratch surfaces or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=smash&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>smash</strong></a> fragile parts.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工人须固定保护罩、轻轻松开配件，并避免划伤表面或打碎易碎零件。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">smash a window</span> · 砸窗</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=repair&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">repair</a></span> · 维修</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=surface%20protection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">surface protection</a></span> · 表面保护</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-069">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.擦破，刮坏；搔；n.乱涂；划痕，擦伤</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Fasten the frame carefully, loosen tight screws and avoid tools that might <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=scratch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>scratch</strong></a> the glass or smash it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">仔细固定框架，拧松过紧螺丝，并避免使用可能划伤或砸碎玻璃的工具。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If you loosen the hinge before you fasten the door, it may swing and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=scratch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>scratch</strong></a> or smash the mirror.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">若固定门之前先松开铰链，门可能摆动，划伤或撞碎镜子。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Workers must fasten protective covers, loosen fittings gently and avoid actions that <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=scratch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>scratch</strong></a> surfaces or smash fragile parts.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工人须固定保护罩、轻轻松开配件，并避免划伤表面或打碎易碎零件。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">scratch a surface</span> · 划伤表面</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=repair&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">repair</a></span> · 维修</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=surface%20protection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">surface protection</a></span> · 表面保护</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-070">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-070" class="vocabulary-word-title">wipe<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-070" aria-label="定位 wipe">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-070" data-mark-label="wipe" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="wipe：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="wipe：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wipe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 wipe" title="在有道查询 wipe">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-070</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.揩，擦</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Restorers split damaged panels, peel away loose layers, scrape old paint, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wipe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wipe</strong></a> the wood and polish it.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">修复人员拆开损坏板材、剥掉松层、刮除旧漆、擦拭木材并抛光。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you split the board, peel off its label, scrape away glue, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wipe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wipe</strong></a> it clean and polish the visible edge.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">劈开木板前，先剥去标签、刮掉胶、擦干净，再抛光可见边缘。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The workshop taught us to peel damaged veneer, split replacement wood, scrape rough edges, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wipe&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wipe</strong></a> dust away and polish the surface.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">作坊教我们剥除损坏饰面、劈开替换木材、刮平粗边、擦去灰尘并抛光表面。</p></li>
</ol>

### 词群 20.07｜物体怎样运动与连接 {#group-20-07}

**串联方式：** 摇摆、震动、旋转之后发生碰撞和接触，再连接、组合、包裹、穿透、插入及拖曳。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-075">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-075" class="vocabulary-word-title">sway<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-075" aria-label="定位 sway">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-075" data-mark-label="sway" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="sway：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="sway：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sway&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 sway" title="在有道查询 sway">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-075</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V. (使)摇摆，(使)摆动，（使）摇晃</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Visitors can watch leaves <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sway&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sway</strong></a>, branches shake, engines vibrate, turbines rotate and pinwheels whirl.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">访客能观察树叶摇摆、树枝晃动、引擎振动、涡轮转动和风车旋转。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Strong winds make the mast <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sway&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sway</strong></a> and shake, cause cables to vibrate and let loose vanes rotate or whirl.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">强风使桅杆摇摆晃动、缆绳振动，并让松动叶片转动或飞转。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibit shows how objects <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sway&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sway</strong></a>, shake and vibrate rather than simply rotate or whirl around an axis.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览展示物体如何摆动、摇晃和振动，而非仅绕轴转动或旋转。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">sway in the wind</span> · 随风摇摆</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=motion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">motion</a></span> · 运动</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mechanics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">mechanics</a></span> · 力学</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-076">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-076" class="vocabulary-word-title">shake<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-076" aria-label="定位 shake">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-076" data-mark-label="shake" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="shake：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="shake：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 shake" title="在有道查询 shake">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-076</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.摇动，(使)颤动；n.摇动，颤动</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Visitors can watch leaves sway, branches <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shake</strong></a>, engines vibrate, turbines rotate and pinwheels whirl.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">访客能观察树叶摇摆、树枝晃动、引擎振动、涡轮转动和风车旋转。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Strong winds make the mast sway and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shake</strong></a>, cause cables to vibrate and let loose vanes rotate or whirl.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">强风使桅杆摇摆晃动、缆绳振动，并让松动叶片转动或飞转。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibit shows how objects sway, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shake</strong></a> and vibrate rather than simply rotate or whirl around an axis.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览展示物体如何摆动、摇晃和振动，而非仅绕轴转动或旋转。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">shake a bottle</span> · 摇瓶子</p>

</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-077">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-077" class="vocabulary-word-title">vibrate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-077" aria-label="定位 vibrate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-077" data-mark-label="vibrate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="vibrate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="vibrate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vibrate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 vibrate" title="在有道查询 vibrate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-077</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V. (使)振动，(使)摇摆</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Visitors can watch leaves sway, branches shake, engines <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vibrate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vibrate</strong></a>, turbines rotate and pinwheels whirl.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">访客能观察树叶摇摆、树枝晃动、引擎振动、涡轮转动和风车旋转。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Strong winds make the mast sway and shake, cause cables to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vibrate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vibrate</strong></a> and let loose vanes rotate or whirl.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">强风使桅杆摇摆晃动、缆绳振动，并让松动叶片转动或飞转。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibit shows how objects sway, shake and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vibrate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>vibrate</strong></a> rather than simply rotate or whirl around an axis.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览展示物体如何摆动、摇晃和振动，而非仅绕轴转动或旋转。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">vibrate gently</span> · 轻轻振动</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=motion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">motion</a></span> · 运动</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mechanics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">mechanics</a></span> · 力学</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-078">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-078" class="vocabulary-word-title">whirl<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-078" aria-label="定位 whirl">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-078" data-mark-label="whirl" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="whirl：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="whirl：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=whirl&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 whirl" title="在有道查询 whirl">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-078</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V. (使)回旋n回旋</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Visitors can watch leaves sway, branches shake, engines vibrate, turbines rotate and pinwheels <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=whirl&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>whirl</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">访客能观察树叶摇摆、树枝晃动、引擎振动、涡轮转动和风车旋转。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Strong winds make the mast sway and shake, cause cables to vibrate and let loose vanes rotate or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=whirl&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>whirl</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">强风使桅杆摇摆晃动、缆绳振动，并让松动叶片转动或飞转。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibit shows how objects sway, shake and vibrate rather than simply rotate or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=whirl&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>whirl</strong></a> around an axis.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览展示物体如何摆动、摇晃和振动，而非仅绕轴转动或旋转。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">whirl around</span> · 旋转</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=motion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">motion</a></span> · 运动</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mechanics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">mechanics</a></span> · 力学</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-079">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-079" class="vocabulary-word-title">rotate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-079" aria-label="定位 rotate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-079" data-mark-label="rotate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="rotate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="rotate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rotate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 rotate" title="在有道查询 rotate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-079</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. (使)旋转，(使)转动</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Strong winds make the mast sway and shake, cause cables to vibrate and let loose vanes <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rotate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rotate</strong></a> or whirl.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">强风使桅杆摇摆晃动、缆绳振动，并让松动叶片转动或飞转。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibit shows how objects sway, shake and vibrate rather than simply <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rotate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rotate</strong></a> or whirl around an axis.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览展示物体如何摆动、摇晃和振动，而非仅绕轴转动或旋转。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">rotate slowly</span> · 缓慢转动</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=motion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">motion</a></span> · 运动</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mechanics&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">mechanics</a></span> · 力学</li></ul>
