<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-080">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-080" class="vocabulary-word-title">shuffle<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-080" aria-label="定位 shuffle">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-080" data-mark-label="shuffle" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="shuffle：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="shuffle：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shuffle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 shuffle" title="在有道查询 shuffle">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-080</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v洗(牌)；拖着脚走；搅乱.......的次序；n.洗牌</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As commuters <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shuffle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shuffle</strong></a> forward, they may collide or make unwanted contact in the crowded station.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">通勤者拖步向前时，可能在拥挤车站相撞或发生不愿有的接触。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shuffle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shuffle</strong></a> into the crossing until you make eye contact with drivers and know vehicles will not collide with you.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要拖步进入路口，直到与司机目光接触并确定车辆不会撞上你。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The simulation shows particles <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=shuffle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>shuffle</strong></a> through a narrow channel, make contact and collide.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">模拟展示粒子在窄通道中挪动、接触并碰撞。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">shuffle your feet</span> · 拖着脚走</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crowding&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">crowding</a></span> · 拥挤</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=collision&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">collision</a></span> · 碰撞</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-081">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-081" class="vocabulary-word-title">collide<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-081" aria-label="定位 collide">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-081" data-mark-label="collide" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="collide：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="collide：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=collide&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 collide" title="在有道查询 collide">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-081</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.碰撞；抵触</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As commuters shuffle forward, they may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=collide&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>collide</strong></a> or make unwanted contact in the crowded station.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">通勤者拖步向前时，可能在拥挤车站相撞或发生不愿有的接触。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not shuffle into the crossing until you make eye contact with drivers and know vehicles will not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=collide&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>collide</strong></a> with you.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要拖步进入路口，直到与司机目光接触并确定车辆不会撞上你。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The simulation shows particles shuffle through a narrow channel, make contact and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=collide&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>collide</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">模拟展示粒子在窄通道中挪动、接触并碰撞。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">collide with a vehicle</span> · 与车辆相撞</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crowding&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">crowding</a></span> · 拥挤</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=collision&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">collision</a></span> · 碰撞</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-082">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-082" class="vocabulary-word-title">contact<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-082" aria-label="定位 contact">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-082" data-mark-label="contact" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="contact：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="contact：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contact&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 contact" title="在有道查询 contact">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-082</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.接触，联系；v.接触，联系</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As commuters shuffle forward, they may collide or make unwanted <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contact&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>contact</strong></a> in the crowded station.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">通勤者拖步向前时，可能在拥挤车站相撞或发生不愿有的接触。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not shuffle into the crossing until you make eye <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contact&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>contact</strong></a> with drivers and know vehicles will not collide with you.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要拖步进入路口，直到与司机目光接触并确定车辆不会撞上你。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The simulation shows particles shuffle through a narrow channel, make <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contact&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>contact</strong></a> and collide.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">模拟展示粒子在窄通道中挪动、接触并碰撞。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">make contact</span> · 接触</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=crowding&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">crowding</a></span> · 拥挤</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=collision&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">collision</a></span> · 碰撞</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-083">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-083" class="vocabulary-word-title">connect<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-083" aria-label="定位 connect">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-083" data-mark-label="connect" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="connect：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="connect：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=connect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 connect" title="在有道查询 connect">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-083</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.(使)连接；(使)接通(电源等)</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Shared projects <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=connect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>connect</strong></a> residents, combine their skills, create a bond and integrate newcomers into the community.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">共同项目连接居民、汇集技能、建立纽带，并让新来者融入社区。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The programme will integrate local services, combine budgets and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=connect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>connect</strong></a> teams through a stronger professional bond.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">项目将整合本地服务、合并预算，并通过更强的专业纽带连接团队。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Teachers combine activities that <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=connect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>connect</strong></a> ideas, strengthen the classroom bond and integrate language with science.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">老师结合能联系概念、加强课堂纽带并整合语言与科学的活动。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">connect two devices</span> · 连接两台设备</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=integration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">integration</a></span> · 整合</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=collaboration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">collaboration</a></span> · 合作</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-084">

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Shared projects connect residents, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=combine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>combine</strong></a> their skills, create a bond and integrate newcomers into the community.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">共同项目连接居民、汇集技能、建立纽带，并让新来者融入社区。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The programme will integrate local services, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=combine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>combine</strong></a> budgets and connect teams through a stronger professional bond.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">项目将整合本地服务、合并预算，并通过更强的专业纽带连接团队。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Teachers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=combine&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>combine</strong></a> activities that connect ideas, strengthen the classroom bond and integrate language with science.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">老师结合能联系概念、加强课堂纽带并整合语言与科学的活动。</p></li>
</ol>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.裹，包；用......缠绕</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Workers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wrap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wrap</strong></a> the container, insert a protective sheet and tilt it carefully so sharp contents cannot pierce the lining or penetrate the outer layer.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工人包好容器、插入保护片，并小心倾斜，避免尖锐内容物刺破内衬或穿透外层。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wrap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wrap</strong></a> the package, insert padding, check that no point can pierce or penetrate it, and tilt it to test stability.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">包装前，放入衬垫、检查尖端不能刺穿或穿透包装，并倾斜测试稳定性。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not tilt the box until you insert a divider and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wrap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wrap</strong></a> objects that might pierce the cardboard and penetrate the cover.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">先插入隔板并包好可能刺破纸板、穿透盖子的物体，再倾斜盒子。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">wrap a parcel</span> · 包裹包裹</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=packaging&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">packaging</a></span> · 包装</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=protection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">protection</a></span> · 保护</li></ul>

</details>

<summary><h4 id="word-20-091" class="vocabulary-word-title">tilt<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-091" aria-label="定位 tilt">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-091" data-mark-label="tilt" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="tilt：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="tilt：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tilt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 tilt" title="在有道查询 tilt">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-091</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V. (使)倾斜，(使)歪斟</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Workers wrap the container, insert a protective sheet and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tilt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tilt</strong></a> it carefully so sharp contents cannot pierce the lining or penetrate the outer layer.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工人包好容器、插入保护片，并小心倾斜，避免尖锐内容物刺破内衬或穿透外层。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you wrap the package, insert padding, check that no point can pierce or penetrate it, and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tilt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tilt</strong></a> it to test stability.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">包装前，放入衬垫、检查尖端不能刺穿或穿透包装，并倾斜测试稳定性。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tilt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tilt</strong></a> the box until you insert a divider and wrap objects that might pierce the cardboard and penetrate the cover.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">先插入隔板并包好可能刺破纸板、穿透盖子的物体，再倾斜盒子。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">tilt a container</span> · 倾斜容器</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=packaging&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">packaging</a></span> · 包装</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=protection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">protection</a></span> · 保护</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-092">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-092" class="vocabulary-word-title">tow<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-092" aria-label="定位 tow">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-092" data-mark-label="tow" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="tow：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="tow：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 tow" title="在有道查询 tow">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-092</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v拖，控，牵引；n.牵引</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The truck will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tow</strong></a> the damaged car, which has left a trail of oil.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">卡车将拖走受损汽车，汽车留下了一道油迹。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We followed the tyre trail to find the van we needed to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tow</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们沿轮胎痕迹找到需要拖走的货车。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A rescue boat can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tow&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tow</strong></a> the kayak while rescuers watch its trail in the water.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">救援船可拖曳皮艇，同时救援者观察其水中尾迹。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">tow a vehicle</span> · 拖车</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recovery%20vehicle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">recovery vehicle</a></span> · 救援拖车</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tracking&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">tracking</a></span> · 追踪</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-093">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-093" class="vocabulary-word-title">trail<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-093" aria-label="定位 trail">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-093" data-mark-label="trail" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="trail：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="trail：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trail&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 trail" title="在有道查询 trail">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-093</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We followed the tyre <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trail&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trail</strong></a> to find the van we needed to tow.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们沿轮胎痕迹找到需要拖走的货车。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A rescue boat can tow the kayak while rescuers watch its <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trail&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trail</strong></a> in the water.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">救援船可拖曳皮艇，同时救援者观察其水中尾迹。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">leave a trail</span> · 留下痕迹</p>

</div>

### 词群 20.08｜安排与操控 {#group-20-08}

**串联方式：** 先安排对象，再操作、操纵或控制方向；留意 manipulate 可带负面含义。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-094">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-094" class="vocabulary-word-title">arrange<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-094" aria-label="定位 arrange">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-094" data-mark-label="arrange" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="arrange：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="arrange：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arrange&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 arrange" title="在有道查询 arrange">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-094</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.安排，整理，使有条理</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The instructor will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arrange&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>arrange</strong></a> lessons so beginners can manipulate the controls and steer the boat safely.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练将安排课程，让初学者能操作控制装置并安全驾驶小船。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arrange&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>arrange</strong></a> the chairs to give each trainee room to manipulate the simulator and steer around obstacles.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们安排座椅，让每位学员有空间操作模拟器并绕开障碍驾驶。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Managers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arrange&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>arrange</strong></a> support for staff learning to manipulate new software and steer a project through change.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">经理为学习操作新软件、引导项目度过变革的员工安排支持。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">arrange a meeting</span> · 安排会议</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=control&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">control</a></span> · 控制</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=instruction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">instruction</a></span> · 指导</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-095">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-095" class="vocabulary-word-title">manipulate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-095" aria-label="定位 manipulate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-095" data-mark-label="manipulate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="manipulate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="manipulate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=manipulate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 manipulate" title="在有道查询 manipulate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-095</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V. (熟练地操作；操纵</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The instructor will arrange lessons so beginners can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=manipulate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>manipulate</strong></a> the controls and steer the boat safely.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练将安排课程，让初学者能操作控制装置并安全驾驶小船。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We arrange the chairs to give each trainee room to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=manipulate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>manipulate</strong></a> the simulator and steer around obstacles.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们安排座椅，让每位学员有空间操作模拟器并绕开障碍驾驶。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Managers arrange support for staff learning to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=manipulate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>manipulate</strong></a> new software and steer a project through change.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">经理为学习操作新软件、引导项目度过变革的员工安排支持。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">manipulate a device</span> · 操作设备</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=control&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">control</a></span> · 控制</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=instruction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">instruction</a></span> · 指导</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-096">

### 词群 20.09｜仪式、押注与承诺 {#group-20-09}

**串联方式：** 先认洗礼仪式，再比较打赌、押注和赌博；promise 单独接到作出承诺。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-097">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-097" class="vocabulary-word-title">baptise<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-097" aria-label="定位 baptise">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-097" data-mark-label="baptise" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="baptise：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="baptise：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=baptise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 baptise" title="在有道查询 baptise">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-097</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">( = baptize )；v.给......施行洗礼(或浸礼)</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The church will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=baptise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>baptise</strong></a> the child on Sunday.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教堂将于周日为孩子施洗。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Families gathered to watch the minister <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=baptise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>baptise</strong></a> the infant.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">家人聚集观看牧师为婴儿施洗。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The museum explains why some Christian communities <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=baptise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>baptise</strong></a> adults rather than babies.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆解释某些基督教群体为何为成人而非婴儿施洗。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">baptise a child</span> · 为孩子施洗</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=baptism&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">baptism</a></span> · 洗礼</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=religious%20ceremony&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">religious ceremony</a></span> · 宗教仪式</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-098">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-098" class="vocabulary-word-title">bet<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-098" aria-label="定位 bet">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-098" data-mark-label="bet" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="bet：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="bet：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 bet" title="在有道查询 bet">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-098</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.下注，打赌；敢说；n.打赌；赌注</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">He made a promise not to gamble because a single <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bet</strong></a> could put his savings at stake.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他承诺不赌博，因为一次下注就可能危及积蓄。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">With the family's future at stake, she refused to gamble or place a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bet</strong></a> despite a friend's promise of easy money.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">家庭未来面临风险，她拒绝赌博或下注，尽管朋友承诺轻松赚钱。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A promise of profit can tempt people to gamble, even when they cannot afford the stake required for a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bet</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">获利承诺可能诱使人赌博，即使他们负担不起下注所需赌资。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">place a bet</span> · 下注</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=financial%20risk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">financial risk</a></span> · 财务风险</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=addiction&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">addiction</a></span> · 成瘾</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">He made a promise not to gamble because a single bet could put his savings at <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stake</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他承诺不赌博，因为一次下注就可能危及积蓄。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">With the family's future at <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stake</strong></a>, she refused to gamble or place a bet despite a friend's promise of easy money.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">家庭未来面临风险，她拒绝赌博或下注，尽管朋友承诺轻松赚钱。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A promise of profit can tempt people to gamble, even when they cannot afford the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stake</strong></a> required for a bet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">获利承诺可能诱使人赌博，即使他们负担不起下注所需赌资。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">at stake</span> · 有风险</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-100">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.赌博；冒险；n.赌博；冒险</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">He made a promise not to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gamble&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gamble</strong></a> because a single bet could put his savings at stake.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他承诺不赌博，因为一次下注就可能危及积蓄。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">With the family's future at stake, she refused to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gamble&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gamble</strong></a> or place a bet despite a friend's promise of easy money.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">家庭未来面临风险，她拒绝赌博或下注，尽管朋友承诺轻松赚钱。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A promise of profit can tempt people to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gamble&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gamble</strong></a>, even when they cannot afford the stake required for a bet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">获利承诺可能诱使人赌博，即使他们负担不起下注所需赌资。</p></li>
</ol>

### 词群 20.10｜行为怎样引发情绪 {#group-20-10}

**串联方式：** 从吸引、着迷到排斥、欺凌、惊讶、烦恼和困惑；讽刺、侮辱与轻蔑是互动语气。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-102">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-102" class="vocabulary-word-title">attract<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-102" aria-label="定位 attract">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-102" data-mark-label="attract" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="attract：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="attract：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attract&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 attract" title="在有道查询 attract">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-102</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.吸引；引起</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The film can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attract&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>attract</strong></a> viewers and mesmerise them, but its nightmare scenes may make some obsess over frightening images.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">影片能吸引并迷住观众，但噩梦场景可能让一些人反复想着恐怖画面。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An advertisement designed to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attract&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>attract</strong></a> and mesmerise children may encourage them to obsess over a toy and create a nightmare for parents.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">旨在吸引并迷住孩子的广告，可能让他们痴迷玩具，给父母带来噩梦般的麻烦。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The director hoped to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attract&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>attract</strong></a> audiences with a nightmare story that would mesmerise them rather than make them obsess over special effects.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">导演希望用噩梦故事吸引并迷住观众，而不是让他们只痴迷特效。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">attract attention</span> · 吸引注意</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attention&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">attention</a></span> · 注意力</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fascination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">fascination</a></span> · 着迷</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-103">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-103" class="vocabulary-word-title">obsess<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-103" aria-label="定位 obsess">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-103" data-mark-label="obsess" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="obsess：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="obsess：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=obsess&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 obsess" title="在有道查询 obsess">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-103</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. (使)痴迷，(使)心神不宁</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The film can attract viewers and mesmerise them, but its nightmare scenes may make some <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=obsess&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>obsess</strong></a> over frightening images.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">影片能吸引并迷住观众，但噩梦场景可能让一些人反复想着恐怖画面。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An advertisement designed to attract and mesmerise children may encourage them to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=obsess&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>obsess</strong></a> over a toy and create a nightmare for parents.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">旨在吸引并迷住孩子的广告，可能让他们痴迷玩具，给父母带来噩梦般的麻烦。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The director hoped to attract audiences with a nightmare story that would mesmerise them rather than make them <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=obsess&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>obsess</strong></a> over special effects.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">导演希望用噩梦故事吸引并迷住观众，而不是让他们只痴迷特效。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">obsess over details</span> · 沉迷细节</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attention&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">attention</a></span> · 注意力</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fascination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">fascination</a></span> · 着迷</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-104">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-104" class="vocabulary-word-title">mesmerise<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-104" aria-label="定位 mesmerise">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-104" data-mark-label="mesmerise" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="mesmerise：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="mesmerise：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mesmerise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 mesmerise" title="在有道查询 mesmerise">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-104</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">( = mesmerize)；v.吸引，迷住</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The film can attract viewers and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mesmerise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mesmerise</strong></a> them, but its nightmare scenes may make some obsess over frightening images.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">影片能吸引并迷住观众，但噩梦场景可能让一些人反复想着恐怖画面。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An advertisement designed to attract and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mesmerise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mesmerise</strong></a> children may encourage them to obsess over a toy and create a nightmare for parents.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">旨在吸引并迷住孩子的广告，可能让他们痴迷玩具，给父母带来噩梦般的麻烦。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The director hoped to attract audiences with a nightmare story that would <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mesmerise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mesmerise</strong></a> them rather than make them obsess over special effects.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">导演希望用噩梦故事吸引并迷住观众，而不是让他们只痴迷特效。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">mesmerise an audience</span> · 迷住观众</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attention&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">attention</a></span> · 注意力</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fascination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">fascination</a></span> · 着迷</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-105">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-105" class="vocabulary-word-title">nightmare<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-105" aria-label="定位 nightmare">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-105" data-mark-label="nightmare" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="nightmare：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="nightmare：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nightmare&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 nightmare" title="在有道查询 nightmare">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-105</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.噩梦，梦魔</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The film can attract viewers and mesmerise them, but its <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nightmare&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>nightmare</strong></a> scenes may make some obsess over frightening images.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">影片能吸引并迷住观众，但噩梦场景可能让一些人反复想着恐怖画面。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An advertisement designed to attract and mesmerise children may encourage them to obsess over a toy and create a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nightmare&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>nightmare</strong></a> for parents.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">旨在吸引并迷住孩子的广告，可能让他们痴迷玩具，给父母带来噩梦般的麻烦。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The director hoped to attract audiences with a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nightmare&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>nightmare</strong></a> story that would mesmerise them rather than make them obsess over special effects.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">导演希望用噩梦故事吸引并迷住观众，而不是让他们只痴迷特效。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a recurring nightmare</span> · 反复的噩梦</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attention&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">attention</a></span> · 注意力</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fascination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">fascination</a></span> · 着迷</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-106">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-106" class="vocabulary-word-title">expel<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-106" aria-label="定位 expel">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-106" data-mark-label="expel" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="expel：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="expel：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=expel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 expel" title="在有道查询 expel">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-106</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.驱逐；开除；排出</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Schools may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=expel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>expel</strong></a> pupils who repeatedly bully classmates.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学校可能开除反复欺负同学的学生。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before deciding to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=expel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>expel</strong></a> a student, teachers must investigate claims that others bully him.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">决定开除学生前，老师须调查他遭他人欺负的说法。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A policy to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=expel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>expel</strong></a> offenders should accompany efforts to prevent pupils from learning to bully.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">开除违规者的政策应配合防止学生学会欺凌的努力。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">expel a pupil</span> · 开除学生</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=school%20discipline&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">school discipline</a></span> · 学校纪律</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bullying%20prevention&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">bullying prevention</a></span> · 霸凌预防</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-107">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-107" class="vocabulary-word-title">bully<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-107" aria-label="定位 bully">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-107" data-mark-label="bully" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="bully：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="bully：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bully&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 bully" title="在有道查询 bully">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-107</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 恐吓，欺凌；n. 恃强凌弱者</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Schools may expel pupils who repeatedly <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bully&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bully</strong></a> classmates.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学校可能开除反复欺负同学的学生。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before deciding to expel a student, teachers must investigate claims that others <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bully&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bully</strong></a> him.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">决定开除学生前，老师须调查他遭他人欺负的说法。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A policy to expel offenders should accompany efforts to prevent pupils from learning to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bully&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bully</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">开除违规者的政策应配合防止学生学会欺凌的努力。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">bully a classmate</span> · 欺负同学</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=school%20discipline&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">school discipline</a></span> · 学校纪律</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bullying%20prevention&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">bullying prevention</a></span> · 霸凌预防</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-108">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-108" class="vocabulary-word-title">amaze<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-108" aria-label="定位 amaze">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-108" data-mark-label="amaze" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="amaze：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="amaze：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=amaze&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 amaze" title="在有道查询 amaze">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-108</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.使惊奇，使惊愕</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibition will involve visitors in experiments that <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=amaze&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>amaze</strong></a> and astonish them, inviting everyone to marvel at science.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览将让访客参与令人惊叹、惊讶的实验，邀请大家感受科学的奇妙。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Lessons that involve outdoor discovery can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=amaze&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>amaze</strong></a> children, astonish parents and encourage both to marvel at nature.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">包含户外发现的课程能让孩子惊叹、让父母惊讶，并鼓励双方惊叹自然。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The performance aims to involve the audience, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=amaze&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>amaze</strong></a> critics, astonish newcomers and make experienced musicians marvel at its precision.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">表演旨在让观众参与、令评论家惊叹、让新观众惊讶，并使资深音乐家赞叹其精准。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">amaze visitors</span> · 让游客惊叹</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wonder&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">wonder</a></span> · 惊奇</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=engagement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">engagement</a></span> · 参与</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-109">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-109" class="vocabulary-word-title">marvel<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-109" aria-label="定位 marvel">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-109" data-mark-label="marvel" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="marvel：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="marvel：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=marvel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 marvel" title="在有道查询 marvel">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-109</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V感到惊讶，大为赞叹；n.奇迹</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibition will involve visitors in experiments that amaze and astonish them, inviting everyone to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=marvel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>marvel</strong></a> at science.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览将让访客参与令人惊叹、惊讶的实验，邀请大家感受科学的奇妙。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Lessons that involve outdoor discovery can amaze children, astonish parents and encourage both to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=marvel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>marvel</strong></a> at nature.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">包含户外发现的课程能让孩子惊叹、让父母惊讶，并鼓励双方惊叹自然。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The performance aims to involve the audience, amaze critics, astonish newcomers and make experienced musicians <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=marvel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>marvel</strong></a> at its precision.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">表演旨在让观众参与、令评论家惊叹、让新观众惊讶，并使资深音乐家赞叹其精准。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">marvel at nature</span> · 惊叹自然</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wonder&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">wonder</a></span> · 惊奇</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=engagement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">engagement</a></span> · 参与</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<summary><h4 id="word-20-110" class="vocabulary-word-title">astonish<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-110" aria-label="定位 astonish">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-110" data-mark-label="astonish" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="astonish：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="astonish：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=astonish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 astonish" title="在有道查询 astonish">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-110</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.使惊讶</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibition will involve visitors in experiments that amaze and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=astonish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>astonish</strong></a> them, inviting everyone to marvel at science.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览将让访客参与令人惊叹、惊讶的实验，邀请大家感受科学的奇妙。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Lessons that involve outdoor discovery can amaze children, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=astonish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>astonish</strong></a> parents and encourage both to marvel at nature.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">包含户外发现的课程能让孩子惊叹、让父母惊讶，并鼓励双方惊叹自然。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The performance aims to involve the audience, amaze critics, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=astonish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>astonish</strong></a> newcomers and make experienced musicians marvel at its precision.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">表演旨在让观众参与、令评论家惊叹、让新观众惊讶，并使资深音乐家赞叹其精准。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">astonish an audience</span> · 使观众惊讶</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wonder&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">wonder</a></span> · 惊奇</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=engagement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">engagement</a></span> · 参与</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-111">

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. (使)参加；牵涉</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibition will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=involve&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>involve</strong></a> visitors in experiments that amaze and astonish them, inviting everyone to marvel at science.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览将让访客参与令人惊叹、惊讶的实验，邀请大家感受科学的奇妙。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Lessons that <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=involve&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>involve</strong></a> outdoor discovery can amaze children, astonish parents and encourage both to marvel at nature.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">包含户外发现的课程能让孩子惊叹、让父母惊讶，并鼓励双方惊叹自然。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The performance aims to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=involve&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>involve</strong></a> the audience, amaze critics, astonish newcomers and make experienced musicians marvel at its precision.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">表演旨在让观众参与、令评论家惊叹、让新观众惊讶，并使资深音乐家赞叹其精准。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">involve participants</span> · 让参与者参与</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wonder&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">wonder</a></span> · 惊奇</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=engagement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">engagement</a></span> · 参与</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-112">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-112" class="vocabulary-word-title">annoy<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-112" aria-label="定位 annoy">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-112" data-mark-label="annoy" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="annoy：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="annoy：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=annoy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 annoy" title="在有道查询 annoy">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-112</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.使恼怒；打扰</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unclear rules can bewilder visitors, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=annoy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>annoy</strong></a> staff and upset residents.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不明确的规则会让访客困惑、员工烦恼、居民不快。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The sudden change may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=annoy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>annoy</strong></a> customers, upset regular users and bewilder new staff.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">突然改变可能让顾客烦恼、老用户不快，并使新员工困惑。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not let confusing messages bewilder the team, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=annoy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>annoy</strong></a> volunteers or upset people waiting for help.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">别让混乱信息使团队困惑、志愿者烦恼，或让等待援助的人不快。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">annoy a neighbour</span> · 使邻居烦恼</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=confusion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">confusion</a></span> · 困惑</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=frustration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">frustration</a></span> · 挫败感</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-113">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-113" class="vocabulary-word-title">upset<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-113" aria-label="定位 upset">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-113" data-mark-label="upset" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="upset：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="upset：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=upset&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 upset" title="在有道查询 upset">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-113</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.使心烦意乱，使生气；搅乱；打翻</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unclear rules can bewilder visitors, annoy staff and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=upset&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>upset</strong></a> residents.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不明确的规则会让访客困惑、员工烦恼、居民不快。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The sudden change may annoy customers, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=upset&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>upset</strong></a> regular users and bewilder new staff.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">突然改变可能让顾客烦恼、老用户不快，并使新员工困惑。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not let confusing messages bewilder the team, annoy volunteers or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=upset&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>upset</strong></a> people waiting for help.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">别让混乱信息使团队困惑、志愿者烦恼，或让等待援助的人不快。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">upset a friend</span> · 使朋友难过</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=confusion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">confusion</a></span> · 困惑</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=frustration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">frustration</a></span> · 挫败感</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-114">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.使迷惑，使不知所措</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unclear rules can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bewilder&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bewilder</strong></a> visitors, annoy staff and upset residents.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不明确的规则会让访客困惑、员工烦恼、居民不快。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The sudden change may annoy customers, upset regular users and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bewilder&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bewilder</strong></a> new staff.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">突然改变可能让顾客烦恼、老用户不快，并使新员工困惑。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not let confusing messages <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bewilder&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bewilder</strong></a> the team, annoy volunteers or upset people waiting for help.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">别让混乱信息使团队困惑、志愿者烦恼，或让等待援助的人不快。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">bewilder a visitor</span> · 使访客困惑</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=confusion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">confusion</a></span> · 困惑</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=frustration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">frustration</a></span> · 挫败感</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-115">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-115" class="vocabulary-word-title">irony<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-115" aria-label="定位 irony">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-115" data-mark-label="irony" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="irony：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="irony：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=irony&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 irony" title="在有道查询 irony">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-115</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n反话；有讽刺意昧的情形</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=irony&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>irony</strong></a> was that a campaign against contempt subjected its own volunteers to indignity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">讽刺的是，一场反对蔑视的活动却让自己的志愿者遭受侮辱。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The novel uses <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=irony&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>irony</strong></a> to expose the indignity workers suffer when managers show contempt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小说用讽刺揭示经理轻蔑态度下工人遭受的羞辱。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">There is bitter <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=irony&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>irony</strong></a> in demanding respect while treating another person's indignity with contempt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一面要求尊重，一面蔑视他人受到的羞辱，实在令人感到苦涩讽刺。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a sense of irony</span> · 讽刺意味</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dignity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dignity</a></span> · 尊严</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hypocrisy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hypocrisy</a></span> · 虚伪</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-116">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-116" class="vocabulary-word-title">indignity<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-116" aria-label="定位 indignity">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-116" data-mark-label="indignity" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="indignity：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="indignity：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=indignity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 indignity" title="在有道查询 indignity">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-116</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.侮辱，轻蔑</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The irony was that a campaign against contempt subjected its own volunteers to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=indignity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>indignity</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">讽刺的是，一场反对蔑视的活动却让自己的志愿者遭受侮辱。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The novel uses irony to expose the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=indignity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>indignity</strong></a> workers suffer when managers show contempt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小说用讽刺揭示经理轻蔑态度下工人遭受的羞辱。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">There is bitter irony in demanding respect while treating another person's <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=indignity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>indignity</strong></a> with contempt.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一面要求尊重，一面蔑视他人受到的羞辱，实在令人感到苦涩讽刺。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">suffer an indignity</span> · 遭受侮辱</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dignity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dignity</a></span> · 尊严</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hypocrisy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hypocrisy</a></span> · 虚伪</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-117">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-117" class="vocabulary-word-title">contempt<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-117" aria-label="定位 contempt">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-117" data-mark-label="contempt" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="contempt：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="contempt：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contempt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 contempt" title="在有道查询 contempt">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-117</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.轻视，藐视</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The irony was that a campaign against <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contempt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>contempt</strong></a> subjected its own volunteers to indignity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">讽刺的是，一场反对蔑视的活动却让自己的志愿者遭受侮辱。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The novel uses irony to expose the indignity workers suffer when managers show <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contempt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>contempt</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小说用讽刺揭示经理轻蔑态度下工人遭受的羞辱。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">There is bitter irony in demanding respect while treating another person's indignity with <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contempt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>contempt</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一面要求尊重，一面蔑视他人受到的羞辱，实在令人感到苦涩讽刺。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dignity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dignity</a></span> · 尊严</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hypocrisy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hypocrisy</a></span> · 虚伪</li></ul>
