### 词群 20.11｜忽视、偏离与严重侵害 {#group-20-11}

**串联方式：** 由疏忽、无视和偏见进入逃离、恐慌与侵害，最后看介入和阻碍；罪行词主要用于理解相关语境。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-118">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-118" class="vocabulary-word-title">neglect<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-118" aria-label="定位 neglect">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-118" data-mark-label="neglect" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="neglect：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="neglect：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=neglect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 neglect" title="在有道查询 neglect">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-118</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.忽视，忽略；n.忽视，忽略</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Ignorance and bias can lead inspectors to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=neglect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>neglect</strong></a> risks, disregard warnings and deviate from proper procedure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">无知和偏见会使检查员忽视风险、无视警告并偏离正规程序。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We must not let bias or ignorance cause us to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=neglect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>neglect</strong></a> evidence, disregard complaints or deviate from fair standards.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们不能让偏见或无知使自己忽略证据、无视投诉或偏离公平标准。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Staff training addresses ignorance and bias so employees do not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=neglect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>neglect</strong></a> duties, disregard safety or deviate from agreed methods.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">员工培训处理无知与偏见，避免员工忽略职责、无视安全或偏离约定方法。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">neglect a duty</span> · 忽视职责</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=professional%20judgement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">professional judgement</a></span> · 专业判断</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=training&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">training</a></span> · 培训</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-119">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-119" class="vocabulary-word-title">disregard<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-119" aria-label="定位 disregard">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-119" data-mark-label="disregard" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="disregard：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="disregard：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disregard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 disregard" title="在有道查询 disregard">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-119</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.不顾，漠视；n.忽视，漠视</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Ignorance and bias can lead inspectors to neglect risks, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disregard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>disregard</strong></a> warnings and deviate from proper procedure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">无知和偏见会使检查员忽视风险、无视警告并偏离正规程序。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We must not let bias or ignorance cause us to neglect evidence, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disregard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>disregard</strong></a> complaints or deviate from fair standards.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们不能让偏见或无知使自己忽略证据、无视投诉或偏离公平标准。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Staff training addresses ignorance and bias so employees do not neglect duties, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disregard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>disregard</strong></a> safety or deviate from agreed methods.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">员工培训处理无知与偏见，避免员工忽略职责、无视安全或偏离约定方法。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">disregard a warning</span> · 无视警告</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=professional%20judgement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">professional judgement</a></span> · 专业判断</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=training&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">training</a></span> · 培训</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-120">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-120" class="vocabulary-word-title">ignorance<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-120" aria-label="定位 ignorance">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-120" data-mark-label="ignorance" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="ignorance：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="ignorance：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ignorance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 ignorance" title="在有道查询 ignorance">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-120</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.无知，愚昧</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ignorance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Ignorance</strong></a> and bias can lead inspectors to neglect risks, disregard warnings and deviate from proper procedure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">无知和偏见会使检查员忽视风险、无视警告并偏离正规程序。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We must not let bias or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ignorance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ignorance</strong></a> cause us to neglect evidence, disregard complaints or deviate from fair standards.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们不能让偏见或无知使自己忽略证据、无视投诉或偏离公平标准。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Staff training addresses <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ignorance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ignorance</strong></a> and bias so employees do not neglect duties, disregard safety or deviate from agreed methods.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">员工培训处理无知与偏见，避免员工忽略职责、无视安全或偏离约定方法。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">ignorance of the facts</span> · 不了解事实</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=professional%20judgement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">professional judgement</a></span> · 专业判断</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=training&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">training</a></span> · 培训</li></ul>
</div>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-121">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.使有偏见，使偏心；n.偏见，俑，心</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Ignorance and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bias&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bias</strong></a> can lead inspectors to neglect risks, disregard warnings and deviate from proper procedure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">无知和偏见会使检查员忽视风险、无视警告并偏离正规程序。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We must not let <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bias&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bias</strong></a> or ignorance cause us to neglect evidence, disregard complaints or deviate from fair standards.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们不能让偏见或无知使自己忽略证据、无视投诉或偏离公平标准。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Staff training addresses ignorance and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bias&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bias</strong></a> so employees do not neglect duties, disregard safety or deviate from agreed methods.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">员工培训处理无知与偏见，避免员工忽略职责、无视安全或偏离约定方法。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">unconscious bias</span> · 无意识偏见</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=professional%20judgement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">professional judgement</a></span> · 专业判断</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=training&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">training</a></span> · 培训</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-122">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-122" class="vocabulary-word-title">deviate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-122" aria-label="定位 deviate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-122" data-mark-label="deviate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="deviate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="deviate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deviate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 deviate" title="在有道查询 deviate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-122</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.偏离，背离</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Ignorance and bias can lead inspectors to neglect risks, disregard warnings and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deviate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deviate</strong></a> from proper procedure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">无知和偏见会使检查员忽视风险、无视警告并偏离正规程序。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We must not let bias or ignorance cause us to neglect evidence, disregard complaints or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deviate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deviate</strong></a> from fair standards.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们不能让偏见或无知使自己忽略证据、无视投诉或偏离公平标准。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Staff training addresses ignorance and bias so employees do not neglect duties, disregard safety or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=deviate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>deviate</strong></a> from agreed methods.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">员工培训处理无知与偏见，避免员工忽略职责、无视安全或偏离约定方法。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">deviate from a plan</span> · 偏离计划</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=professional%20judgement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">professional judgement</a></span> · 专业判断</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=training&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">training</a></span> · 培训</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-123">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-123" class="vocabulary-word-title">flee<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-123" aria-label="定位 flee">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-123" data-mark-label="flee" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="flee：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="flee：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flee&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 flee" title="在有道查询 flee">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-123</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.逃避，逃离</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Threats of revenge can terrify families, cause panic and force them to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flee&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flee</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报复威胁会让家庭惊恐、引起恐慌并迫使其逃离。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In the novel, rumours of revenge terrify villagers, who <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flee&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flee</strong></a> in panic.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小说中，报复传闻令村民惊恐，他们仓皇逃离。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Police tried to prevent panic as witnesses prepared to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flee&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>flee</strong></a> after messages intended to terrify them threatened revenge.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有人发出恐吓信息威胁报复后，证人准备逃离，警方试图防止恐慌。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">flee a fire</span> · 逃离火灾</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intimidation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">intimidation</a></span> · 恐吓</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=protection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">protection</a></span> · 保护</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-124">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-124" class="vocabulary-word-title">panic<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-124" aria-label="定位 panic">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-124" data-mark-label="panic" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="panic：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="panic：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=panic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 panic" title="在有道查询 panic">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-124</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.惊恐，惊慌；v.(使)惊恐，(使)惊慌</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Threats of revenge can terrify families, cause <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=panic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>panic</strong></a> and force them to flee.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报复威胁会让家庭惊恐、引起恐慌并迫使其逃离。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In the novel, rumours of revenge terrify villagers, who flee in <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=panic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>panic</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小说中，报复传闻令村民惊恐，他们仓皇逃离。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Police tried to prevent <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=panic&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>panic</strong></a> as witnesses prepared to flee after messages intended to terrify them threatened revenge.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有人发出恐吓信息威胁报复后，证人准备逃离，警方试图防止恐慌。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">in a panic</span> · 惊慌地</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intimidation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">intimidation</a></span> · 恐吓</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=protection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">protection</a></span> · 保护</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-125">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-125" class="vocabulary-word-title">terrify<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-125" aria-label="定位 terrify">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-125" data-mark-label="terrify" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="terrify：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="terrify：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=terrify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 terrify" title="在有道查询 terrify">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-125</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.使恐惧，使非常害怕</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Threats of revenge can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=terrify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>terrify</strong></a> families, cause panic and force them to flee.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报复威胁会让家庭惊恐、引起恐慌并迫使其逃离。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In the novel, rumours of revenge <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=terrify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>terrify</strong></a> villagers, who flee in panic.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小说中，报复传闻令村民惊恐，他们仓皇逃离。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Police tried to prevent panic as witnesses prepared to flee after messages intended to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=terrify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>terrify</strong></a> them threatened revenge.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有人发出恐吓信息威胁报复后，证人准备逃离，警方试图防止恐慌。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">terrify residents</span> · 使居民惊恐</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-126">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-126" class="vocabulary-word-title">revenge<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-126" aria-label="定位 revenge">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-126" data-mark-label="revenge" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="revenge：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="revenge：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=revenge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 revenge" title="在有道查询 revenge">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-126</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.报仇，复仇；v.向......报仇</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Threats of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=revenge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>revenge</strong></a> can terrify families, cause panic and force them to flee.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报复威胁会让家庭惊恐、引起恐慌并迫使其逃离。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In the novel, rumours of <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=revenge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>revenge</strong></a> terrify villagers, who flee in panic.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小说中，报复传闻令村民惊恐，他们仓皇逃离。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Police tried to prevent panic as witnesses prepared to flee after messages intended to terrify them threatened <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=revenge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>revenge</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">有人发出恐吓信息威胁报复后，证人准备逃离，警方试图防止恐慌。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">seek revenge</span> · 寻求报复</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intimidation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">intimidation</a></span> · 恐吓</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=protection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">protection</a></span> · 保护</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-127">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-127" class="vocabulary-word-title">kidnap<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-127" aria-label="定位 kidnap">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-127" data-mark-label="kidnap" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="kidnap：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="kidnap：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=kidnap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 kidnap" title="在有道查询 kidnap">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-127</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.绑架，劫持</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Investigators sought evidence to convict suspects who planned to smuggle weapons, hijack a vehicle and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=kidnap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>kidnap</strong></a> its driver.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查人员寻找证据，以将计划走私武器、劫车并绑架司机的嫌疑人定罪。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The fictional case involves attempts to hijack a ship, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=kidnap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>kidnap</strong></a> its captain and smuggle cargo, but prosecutors still need proof to convict.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">虚构案件涉及劫船、绑架船长及走私货物的企图，但检察官仍需证据才能定罪。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Courts cannot convict merely on rumours that someone might smuggle goods, hijack transport or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=kidnap&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>kidnap</strong></a> a rival.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">法院不能仅凭某人可能走私、劫持交通工具或绑架对手的传闻定罪。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">kidnap a passenger</span> · 绑架乘客</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=criminal%20investigation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">criminal investigation</a></span> · 刑事调查</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prosecution&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">prosecution</a></span> · 起诉</li></ul>

</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-131">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-131" class="vocabulary-word-title">strangle<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-131" aria-label="定位 strangle">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-131" data-mark-label="strangle" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="strangle：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="strangle：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=strangle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 strangle" title="在有道查询 strangle">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-131</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.扼杀，压制；勒死，扼死</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report urges outsiders to intervene before armed groups <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=strangle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>strangle</strong></a> local trade, massacre civilians and impede relief, worsening the decimation of communities.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告敦促外界干预，防止武装团体扼杀本地贸易、屠杀平民并妨碍救援，加剧社区的大量损失。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Witnesses asked officials to intervene after a massacre, investigate attempts to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=strangle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>strangle</strong></a> survivors and prevent intimidation from continuing to impede inquiries into the decimation of the village.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">屠杀后，证人要求官员干预、调查勒杀幸存者的企图，并防止恐吓继续阻碍对村庄大量人员死亡的调查。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The documentary shows how officials failed to intervene when forces began to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=strangle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>strangle</strong></a> food supplies and impede aid before a massacre caused the decimation of the settlement.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">纪录片展示军队开始扼制食品供应、阻碍援助时，官员未能干预；随后屠杀造成聚落人口大量死亡。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">strangle a victim</span> · 勒死受害者</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=atrocity&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">atrocity</a></span> · 暴行</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=humanitarian%20intervention&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">humanitarian intervention</a></span> · 人道干预</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-132">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.残杀，屠杀；v.残杀，屠杀</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report urges outsiders to intervene before armed groups strangle local trade, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=massacre&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>massacre</strong></a> civilians and impede relief, worsening the decimation of communities.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告敦促外界干预，防止武装团体扼杀本地贸易、屠杀平民并妨碍救援，加剧社区的大量损失。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Witnesses asked officials to intervene after a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=massacre&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>massacre</strong></a>, investigate attempts to strangle survivors and prevent intimidation from continuing to impede inquiries into the decimation of the village.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">屠杀后，证人要求官员干预、调查勒杀幸存者的企图，并防止恐吓继续阻碍对村庄大量人员死亡的调查。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The documentary shows how officials failed to intervene when forces began to strangle food supplies and impede aid before a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=massacre&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>massacre</strong></a> caused the decimation of the settlement.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">纪录片展示军队开始扼制食品供应、阻碍援助时，官员未能干预；随后屠杀造成聚落人口大量死亡。</p></li>
</ol>

### 词群 20.12｜诱惑怎样产生 {#group-20-12}

**串联方式：** 从吸引物和诱饵进入引诱、促使与诱惑，注意动作对象及语气。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-136">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-136" class="vocabulary-word-title">allure<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-136" aria-label="定位 allure">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-136" data-mark-label="allure" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="allure：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="allure：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=allure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 allure" title="在有道查询 allure">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-136</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 吸引，引诱</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=allure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>allure</strong></a> of easy money can tempt people when scammers use rewards as bait to induce trust.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">骗子把奖励当诱饵诱导信任时，轻松赚钱的诱惑可能吸引人们。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Advertisements use the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=allure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>allure</strong></a> of luxury as bait to tempt shoppers and induce unnecessary purchases.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">广告把奢华的吸引力当诱饵，诱惑购物者，诱导不必要消费。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The lure works because the bait has enough <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=allure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>allure</strong></a> to tempt consumers and induce a quick decision.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这种诱惑有效，是因为诱饵有足够吸引力来打动消费者并诱导快速决定。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">the allure of wealth</span> · 财富的诱惑</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=persuasion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">persuasion</a></span> · 劝诱</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consumer%20behaviour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">consumer behaviour</a></span> · 消费者行为</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-137">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-137" class="vocabulary-word-title">bait<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-137" aria-label="定位 bait">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-137" data-mark-label="bait" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="bait：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="bait：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bait&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 bait" title="在有道查询 bait">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-137</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v,使上当发火；n.诱饵；诱惑物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The allure of easy money can tempt people when scammers use rewards as <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bait&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bait</strong></a> to induce trust.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">骗子把奖励当诱饵诱导信任时，轻松赚钱的诱惑可能吸引人们。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Advertisements use the allure of luxury as <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bait&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bait</strong></a> to tempt shoppers and induce unnecessary purchases.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">广告把奢华的吸引力当诱饵，诱惑购物者，诱导不必要消费。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The lure works because the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=bait&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>bait</strong></a> has enough allure to tempt consumers and induce a quick decision.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这种诱惑有效，是因为诱饵有足够吸引力来打动消费者并诱导快速决定。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">use bait</span> · 使用诱饵</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=persuasion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">persuasion</a></span> · 劝诱</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consumer%20behaviour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">consumer behaviour</a></span> · 消费者行为</li></ul>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Advertisements use the allure of luxury as bait to tempt shoppers and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=induce&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>induce</strong></a> unnecessary purchases.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">广告把奢华的吸引力当诱饵，诱惑购物者，诱导不必要消费。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The lure works because the bait has enough allure to tempt consumers and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=induce&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>induce</strong></a> a quick decision.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这种诱惑有效，是因为诱饵有足够吸引力来打动消费者并诱导快速决定。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">induce a response</span> · 引起回应</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=persuasion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">persuasion</a></span> · 劝诱</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consumer%20behaviour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">consumer behaviour</a></span> · 消费者行为</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-139">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-139" class="vocabulary-word-title">tempt<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-139" aria-label="定位 tempt">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-139" data-mark-label="tempt" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="tempt：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="tempt：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tempt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 tempt" title="在有道查询 tempt">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-139</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 引诱，诱惑；怂恿</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The allure of easy money can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tempt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tempt</strong></a> people when scammers use rewards as bait to induce trust.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">骗子把奖励当诱饵诱导信任时，轻松赚钱的诱惑可能吸引人们。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Advertisements use the allure of luxury as bait to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tempt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tempt</strong></a> shoppers and induce unnecessary purchases.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">广告把奢华的吸引力当诱饵，诱惑购物者，诱导不必要消费。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The lure works because the bait has enough allure to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tempt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tempt</strong></a> consumers and induce a quick decision.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这种诱惑有效，是因为诱饵有足够吸引力来打动消费者并诱导快速决定。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">tempt a customer</span> · 诱惑顾客</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=persuasion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">persuasion</a></span> · 劝诱</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consumer%20behaviour&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">consumer behaviour</a></span> · 消费者行为</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 20.13｜工作怎样分派 {#group-20-13}

**串联方式：** 指定角色、分配任务、分发资源并派遣人员，各动词配合适对象。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-140">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-140" class="vocabulary-word-title">designate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-140" aria-label="定位 designate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-140" data-mark-label="designate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="designate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="designate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=designate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 designate" title="在有道查询 designate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-140</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.指明；选派，指派</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=designate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>designate</strong></a> a leader, assign roles, distribute equipment and despatch the team.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们将指定负责人、分配角色、发放装备并派出团队。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before they despatch volunteers, coordinators <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=designate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>designate</strong></a> shelters, assign routes and distribute maps.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">派遣志愿者前，协调员指定避难所、分配路线并发放地图。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The office must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=designate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>designate</strong></a> a contact, assign each case, distribute funds and despatch urgent letters.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">办公室须指定联系人、分派案件、分配资金并寄发紧急信件。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">designate a leader</span> · 指定负责人</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=coordination&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">coordination</a></span> · 协调</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=allocation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">allocation</a></span> · 分配</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-141">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before they despatch volunteers, coordinators designate shelters, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assign&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>assign</strong></a> routes and distribute maps.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">派遣志愿者前，协调员指定避难所、分配路线并发放地图。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The office must designate a contact, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assign&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>assign</strong></a> each case, distribute funds and despatch urgent letters.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">办公室须指定联系人、分派案件、分配资金并寄发紧急信件。</p></li>

### 词群 20.14｜拆除、隐藏与排除 {#group-20-14}

**串联方式：** 区分分离、撤销、伪装、隐藏、拒绝、排除和逆转，用于描述对既有状态的改变。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-144">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-144" class="vocabulary-word-title">detach<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-144" aria-label="定位 detach">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-144" data-mark-label="detach" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="detach：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="detach：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=detach&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 detach" title="在有道查询 detach">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-144</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. (使)分开，(使)分离；派遣</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To conceal the gift and disguise its shape, undo the old wrapping and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=detach&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>detach</strong></a> the label.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为隐藏礼物并伪装形状，拆开旧包装并撕下标签。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The actor had to undo the fastening, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=detach&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>detach</strong></a> the badge and disguise his coat to conceal his identity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">演员须解开扣件、拆下徽章并伪装外套，以隐藏身份。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The workshop showed how to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=detach&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>detach</strong></a> a panel, undo a latch and conceal repairs without trying to disguise serious damage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">作坊示范如何拆下板材、打开锁扣并隐藏修补痕迹，同时不掩盖严重损坏。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">detach a label</span> · 撕下标签</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=concealment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">concealment</a></span> · 隐蔽</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=repair%20work&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">repair work</a></span> · 维修工作</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-145">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-145" class="vocabulary-word-title">undo<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-145" aria-label="定位 undo">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-145" data-mark-label="undo" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="undo：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="undo：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=undo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 undo" title="在有道查询 undo">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-145</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.解开，打开；取消</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To conceal the gift and disguise its shape, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=undo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>undo</strong></a> the old wrapping and detach the label.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为隐藏礼物并伪装形状，拆开旧包装并撕下标签。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The actor had to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=undo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>undo</strong></a> the fastening, detach the badge and disguise his coat to conceal his identity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">演员须解开扣件、拆下徽章并伪装外套，以隐藏身份。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The workshop showed how to detach a panel, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=undo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>undo</strong></a> a latch and conceal repairs without trying to disguise serious damage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">作坊示范如何拆下板材、打开锁扣并隐藏修补痕迹，同时不掩盖严重损坏。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">undo a knot</span> · 解开结</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=concealment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">concealment</a></span> · 隐蔽</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=repair%20work&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">repair work</a></span> · 维修工作</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-146">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-146" class="vocabulary-word-title">disguise<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-146" aria-label="定位 disguise">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-146" data-mark-label="disguise" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="disguise：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="disguise：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disguise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 disguise" title="在有道查询 disguise">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-146</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.掩饰；假扮，伪装；n.假扮；伪装物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To conceal the gift and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disguise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>disguise</strong></a> its shape, undo the old wrapping and detach the label.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为隐藏礼物并伪装形状，拆开旧包装并撕下标签。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The actor had to undo the fastening, detach the badge and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disguise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>disguise</strong></a> his coat to conceal his identity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">演员须解开扣件、拆下徽章并伪装外套，以隐藏身份。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The workshop showed how to detach a panel, undo a latch and conceal repairs without trying to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=disguise&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>disguise</strong></a> serious damage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">作坊示范如何拆下板材、打开锁扣并隐藏修补痕迹，同时不掩盖严重损坏。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">disguise an object</span> · 伪装物体</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=concealment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">concealment</a></span> · 隐蔽</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=repair%20work&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">repair work</a></span> · 维修工作</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The actor had to undo the fastening, detach the badge and disguise his coat to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conceal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>conceal</strong></a> his identity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">演员须解开扣件、拆下徽章并伪装外套，以隐藏身份。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The workshop showed how to detach a panel, undo a latch and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=conceal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>conceal</strong></a> repairs without trying to disguise serious damage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">作坊示范如何拆下板材、打开锁扣并隐藏修补痕迹，同时不掩盖严重损坏。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-148">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-148" class="vocabulary-word-title">refuse<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-148" aria-label="定位 refuse">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-148" data-mark-label="refuse" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="refuse：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="refuse：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=refuse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 refuse" title="在有道查询 refuse">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-148</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.拒绝；回绝</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The board may reverse its decision to exclude applicants who <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=refuse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>refuse</strong></a> to disclose irrelevant personal details.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">董事会可能推翻排除拒绝透露无关个人信息的申请者的决定。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials cannot simply <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=refuse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>refuse</strong></a> an appeal, exclude new evidence and decline to reverse an error.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">官员不能直接拒绝上诉、排除新证据，并拒绝纠正错误。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If schools <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=refuse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>refuse</strong></a> to reverse unfair rules, they may continue to exclude vulnerable children.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果学校拒绝撤销不公平规则，就可能继续排斥弱势儿童。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">refuse a request</span> · 拒绝请求</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=appeal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">appeal</a></span> · 申诉</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inclusion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">inclusion</a></span> · 包容</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-149">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-149" class="vocabulary-word-title">exclude<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-149" aria-label="定位 exclude">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-149" data-mark-label="exclude" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="exclude：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="exclude：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exclude&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 exclude" title="在有道查询 exclude">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-149</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v把......排除在外；拒</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The board may reverse its decision to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exclude&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>exclude</strong></a> applicants who refuse to disclose irrelevant personal details.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">董事会可能推翻排除拒绝透露无关个人信息的申请者的决定。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials cannot simply refuse an appeal, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exclude&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>exclude</strong></a> new evidence and decline to reverse an error.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">官员不能直接拒绝上诉、排除新证据，并拒绝纠正错误。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If schools refuse to reverse unfair rules, they may continue to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exclude&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>exclude</strong></a> vulnerable children.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果学校拒绝撤销不公平规则，就可能继续排斥弱势儿童。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">exclude a candidate</span> · 排除候选人</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=appeal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">appeal</a></span> · 申诉</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inclusion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">inclusion</a></span> · 包容</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-150">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-150" class="vocabulary-word-title">reverse<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-150" aria-label="定位 reverse">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-150" data-mark-label="reverse" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="reverse：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="reverse：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reverse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 reverse" title="在有道查询 reverse">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-150</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.颠倒，彻底改变；撤销；n.相反的情况；反面；adj.相反的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The board may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reverse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reverse</strong></a> its decision to exclude applicants who refuse to disclose irrelevant personal details.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">董事会可能推翻排除拒绝透露无关个人信息的申请者的决定。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Officials cannot simply refuse an appeal, exclude new evidence and decline to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reverse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reverse</strong></a> an error.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">官员不能直接拒绝上诉、排除新证据，并拒绝纠正错误。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If schools refuse to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reverse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reverse</strong></a> unfair rules, they may continue to exclude vulnerable children.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果学校拒绝撤销不公平规则，就可能继续排斥弱势儿童。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">reverse a decision</span> · 推翻决定</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=appeal&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">appeal</a></span> · 申诉</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inclusion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">inclusion</a></span> · 包容</li></ul>
</div>
