### 词群 20.15｜保证、愿望与求助 {#group-20-15}

**串联方式：** 从保证、承担、规定和说服进入愿望、渴望、求助与救援；itch 可指痒或渴望。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-151">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-151" class="vocabulary-word-title">assure<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-151" aria-label="定位 assure">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-151" data-mark-label="assure" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="assure：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="assure：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 assure" title="在有道查询 assure">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-151</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.向......保证；保证；保险</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>assure</strong></a> clients of support, undertake inspections, stipulate safeguards, convince them with evidence and reassure them about safety.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们能保证支持客户、承担检查、规定保障措施、用证据说服客户，并使其对安全放心。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The contract will stipulate duties we undertake, helping us <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>assure</strong></a> and reassure residents and convince them the work is safe.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">合同将规定我们承担的职责，帮助我们向居民保证、使其安心，并说服他们工程安全。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To convince the board and reassure staff, managers must undertake checks, stipulate clear standards and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>assure</strong></a> everyone of independent oversight.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为说服董事会并使员工安心，经理必须承担检查、规定明确标准，并保证独立监督。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">assure someone of support</span> · 向某人保证支持</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assurance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">assurance</a></span> · 保证</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contract%20terms&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">contract terms</a></span> · 合同条款</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-152">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-152" class="vocabulary-word-title">undertake<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-152" aria-label="定位 undertake">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-152" data-mark-label="undertake" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="undertake：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="undertake：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=undertake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 undertake" title="在有道查询 undertake">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-152</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.承担；许诺，保证</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We can assure clients of support, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=undertake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>undertake</strong></a> inspections, stipulate safeguards, convince them with evidence and reassure them about safety.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们能保证支持客户、承担检查、规定保障措施、用证据说服客户，并使其对安全放心。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The contract will stipulate duties we <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=undertake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>undertake</strong></a>, helping us assure and reassure residents and convince them the work is safe.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">合同将规定我们承担的职责，帮助我们向居民保证、使其安心，并说服他们工程安全。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To convince the board and reassure staff, managers must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=undertake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>undertake</strong></a> checks, stipulate clear standards and assure everyone of independent oversight.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为说服董事会并使员工安心，经理必须承担检查、规定明确标准，并保证独立监督。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">undertake a task</span> · 承担任务</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assurance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">assurance</a></span> · 保证</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contract%20terms&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">contract terms</a></span> · 合同条款</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-153">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-153" class="vocabulary-word-title">stipulate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-153" aria-label="定位 stipulate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-153" data-mark-label="stipulate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="stipulate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="stipulate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stipulate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 stipulate" title="在有道查询 stipulate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-153</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v规定，明确要求；保证</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We can assure clients of support, undertake inspections, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stipulate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stipulate</strong></a> safeguards, convince them with evidence and reassure them about safety.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们能保证支持客户、承担检查、规定保障措施、用证据说服客户，并使其对安全放心。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The contract will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stipulate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stipulate</strong></a> duties we undertake, helping us assure and reassure residents and convince them the work is safe.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">合同将规定我们承担的职责，帮助我们向居民保证、使其安心，并说服他们工程安全。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To convince the board and reassure staff, managers must undertake checks, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stipulate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>stipulate</strong></a> clear standards and assure everyone of independent oversight.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为说服董事会并使员工安心，经理必须承担检查、规定明确标准，并保证独立监督。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">stipulate a condition</span> · 规定条件</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assurance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">assurance</a></span> · 保证</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contract%20terms&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">contract terms</a></span> · 合同条款</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-154">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-154" class="vocabulary-word-title">convince<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-154" aria-label="定位 convince">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-154" data-mark-label="convince" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="convince：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="convince：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=convince&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 convince" title="在有道查询 convince">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-154</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v使确信，使信服；说服</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We can assure clients of support, undertake inspections, stipulate safeguards, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=convince&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>convince</strong></a> them with evidence and reassure them about safety.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们能保证支持客户、承担检查、规定保障措施、用证据说服客户，并使其对安全放心。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The contract will stipulate duties we undertake, helping us assure and reassure residents and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=convince&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>convince</strong></a> them the work is safe.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">合同将规定我们承担的职责，帮助我们向居民保证、使其安心，并说服他们工程安全。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=convince&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>convince</strong></a> the board and reassure staff, managers must undertake checks, stipulate clear standards and assure everyone of independent oversight.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为说服董事会并使员工安心，经理必须承担检查、规定明确标准，并保证独立监督。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">convince an audience</span> · 说服听众</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assurance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">assurance</a></span> · 保证</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contract%20terms&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">contract terms</a></span> · 合同条款</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-155">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-155" class="vocabulary-word-title">reassure<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-155" aria-label="定位 reassure">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-155" data-mark-label="reassure" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="reassure：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="reassure：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reassure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 reassure" title="在有道查询 reassure">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-155</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.使安心，使放心</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We can assure clients of support, undertake inspections, stipulate safeguards, convince them with evidence and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reassure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reassure</strong></a> them about safety.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们能保证支持客户、承担检查、规定保障措施、用证据说服客户，并使其对安全放心。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The contract will stipulate duties we undertake, helping us assure and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reassure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reassure</strong></a> residents and convince them the work is safe.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">合同将规定我们承担的职责，帮助我们向居民保证、使其安心，并说服他们工程安全。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To convince the board and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reassure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>reassure</strong></a> staff, managers must undertake checks, stipulate clear standards and assure everyone of independent oversight.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为说服董事会并使员工安心，经理必须承担检查、规定明确标准，并保证独立监督。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">reassure a customer</span> · 使客户安心</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assurance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">assurance</a></span> · 保证</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contract%20terms&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">contract terms</a></span> · 合同条款</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-156">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-156" class="vocabulary-word-title">wish<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-156" aria-label="定位 wish">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-156" data-mark-label="wish" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="wish：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="wish：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 wish" title="在有道查询 wish">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-156</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.想要(某事发生)；n.希望</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">People may desire adventure and aspire to travel, yet yearn for home and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wish</strong></a> for familiar company.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">人们可能渴望冒险、立志旅行，却也思念家乡、盼望熟人陪伴。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Young residents <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wish</strong></a> for opportunity, desire independence, aspire to meaningful work and yearn for a secure future.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">年轻居民希望获得机会、渴望独立、向往有意义的工作，并期盼安稳未来。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Refugees yearn for safety, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wish</strong></a> for peace, desire dignity and aspire to rebuild their lives.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">难民渴望安全、祈愿和平、希望保有尊严，并立志重建生活。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">wish for peace</span> · 祈愿和平</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aspiration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">aspiration</a></span> · 抱负</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=longing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">longing</a></span> · 渴望</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-157">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-157" class="vocabulary-word-title">aspire<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-157" aria-label="定位 aspire">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-157" data-mark-label="aspire" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="aspire：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="aspire：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aspire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 aspire" title="在有道查询 aspire">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-157</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.有志于，渴望</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">People may desire adventure and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aspire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>aspire</strong></a> to travel, yet yearn for home and wish for familiar company.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">人们可能渴望冒险、立志旅行，却也思念家乡、盼望熟人陪伴。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Young residents wish for opportunity, desire independence, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aspire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>aspire</strong></a> to meaningful work and yearn for a secure future.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">年轻居民希望获得机会、渴望独立、向往有意义的工作，并期盼安稳未来。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Refugees yearn for safety, wish for peace, desire dignity and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aspire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>aspire</strong></a> to rebuild their lives.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">难民渴望安全、祈愿和平、希望保有尊严，并立志重建生活。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">aspire to lead</span> · 立志领导</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aspiration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">aspiration</a></span> · 抱负</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=longing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">longing</a></span> · 渴望</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-158">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-158" class="vocabulary-word-title">desire<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-158" aria-label="定位 desire">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-158" data-mark-label="desire" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="desire：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="desire：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=desire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 desire" title="在有道查询 desire">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-158</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.愿望，欲望；v.渴望，期望</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">People may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=desire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>desire</strong></a> adventure and aspire to travel, yet yearn for home and wish for familiar company.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">人们可能渴望冒险、立志旅行，却也思念家乡、盼望熟人陪伴。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Young residents wish for opportunity, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=desire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>desire</strong></a> independence, aspire to meaningful work and yearn for a secure future.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">年轻居民希望获得机会、渴望独立、向往有意义的工作，并期盼安稳未来。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Refugees yearn for safety, wish for peace, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=desire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>desire</strong></a> dignity and aspire to rebuild their lives.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">难民渴望安全、祈愿和平、希望保有尊严，并立志重建生活。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">desire change</span> · 渴望改变</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aspiration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">aspiration</a></span> · 抱负</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=longing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">longing</a></span> · 渴望</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-159">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-159" class="vocabulary-word-title">yearn<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-159" aria-label="定位 yearn">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-159" data-mark-label="yearn" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="yearn：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="yearn：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=yearn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 yearn" title="在有道查询 yearn">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-159</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.渴望，向往</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">People may desire adventure and aspire to travel, yet <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=yearn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>yearn</strong></a> for home and wish for familiar company.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">人们可能渴望冒险、立志旅行，却也思念家乡、盼望熟人陪伴。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Young residents wish for opportunity, desire independence, aspire to meaningful work and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=yearn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>yearn</strong></a> for a secure future.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">年轻居民希望获得机会、渴望独立、向往有意义的工作，并期盼安稳未来。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Refugees <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=yearn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>yearn</strong></a> for safety, wish for peace, desire dignity and aspire to rebuild their lives.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">难民渴望安全、祈愿和平、希望保有尊严，并立志重建生活。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">yearn for home</span> · 思念家乡</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=aspiration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">aspiration</a></span> · 抱负</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=longing&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">longing</a></span> · 渴望</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-160">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-160" class="vocabulary-word-title">invoke<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-160" aria-label="定位 invoke">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-160" data-mark-label="invoke" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="invoke：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="invoke：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=invoke&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 invoke" title="在有道查询 invoke">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-160</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.唤起(感情等)；向......祈求帮助；恳求</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An itch for adventure does not justify a traveller's attempt to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=invoke&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>invoke</strong></a> public services for an unnecessary rescue.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">冒险冲动并不能成为旅客为不必要救援而要求动用公共服务的正当理由。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The climber felt an itch to explore but hoped never to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=invoke&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>invoke</strong></a> the emergency clause covering rescue.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">登山者很想探索，却希望永远不必启用涵盖救援的紧急条款。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An itch to return to the mountains led him to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=invoke&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>invoke</strong></a> his insurance policy after a difficult rescue.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">重返山间的渴望使他出行，艰难救援后，他援引保单申请理赔。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">invoke a right</span> · 援引权利</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=adventure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">adventure</a></span> · 冒险</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=insurance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">insurance</a></span> · 保险</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-161">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-161" class="vocabulary-word-title">rescue<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-161" aria-label="定位 rescue">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-161" data-mark-label="rescue" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="rescue：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="rescue：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rescue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 rescue" title="在有道查询 rescue">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-161</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.营救，救援；n. 营救，救援物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An itch for adventure does not justify a traveller's attempt to invoke public services for an unnecessary <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rescue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rescue</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">冒险冲动并不能成为旅客为不必要救援而要求动用公共服务的正当理由。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The climber felt an itch to explore but hoped never to invoke the emergency clause covering <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rescue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rescue</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">登山者很想探索，却希望永远不必启用涵盖救援的紧急条款。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An itch to return to the mountains led him to invoke his insurance policy after a difficult <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rescue&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rescue</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">重返山间的渴望使他出行，艰难救援后，他援引保单申请理赔。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">rescue a climber</span> · 救援登山者</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=adventure&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">adventure</a></span> · 冒险</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=insurance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">insurance</a></span> · 保险</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-162">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.发痒；渴望；n.痒；渴望</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=itch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>itch</strong></a> for adventure does not justify a traveller's attempt to invoke public services for an unnecessary rescue.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">冒险冲动并不能成为旅客为不必要救援而要求动用公共服务的正当理由。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The climber felt an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=itch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>itch</strong></a> to explore but hoped never to invoke the emergency clause covering rescue.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">登山者很想探索，却希望永远不必启用涵盖救援的紧急条款。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=itch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>itch</strong></a> to return to the mountains led him to invoke his insurance policy after a difficult rescue.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">重返山间的渴望使他出行，艰难救援后，他援引保单申请理赔。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an itch to travel</span> · 旅行的渴望</p>

### 词群 20.16｜从尝试到解决任务 {#group-20-16}

**串联方式：** 先尝试、努力和完成，再整理范围、启动、探索、运用并应对问题；loom 描述威胁等隐约逼近。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-163">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-163" class="vocabulary-word-title">attempt<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-163" aria-label="定位 attempt">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-163" data-mark-label="attempt" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="attempt：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="attempt：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attempt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 attempt" title="在有道查询 attempt">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-163</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.尝试，试图；n.尝试，企图</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We must strive to improve, make an effort and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attempt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>attempt</strong></a> new methods to fulfil our responsibilities.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们必须力争进步、付出努力并尝试新方法，以履行责任。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Every <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attempt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>attempt</strong></a> requires effort, but teams that strive together are more likely to fulfil their goals.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">每次尝试都需要努力，但共同奋斗的团队更可能实现目标。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students strive to fulfil their potential, and each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attempt&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>attempt</strong></a> deserves recognition for the effort involved.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生努力发挥潜力，每次尝试都因其中的努力而值得认可。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">make an attempt</span> · 尝试</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perseverance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">perseverance</a></span> · 坚持</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=achievement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">achievement</a></span> · 成就</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-164">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-164" class="vocabulary-word-title">strive<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-164" aria-label="定位 strive">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-164" data-mark-label="strive" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="strive：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="strive：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=strive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 strive" title="在有道查询 strive">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-164</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.努力，奋斗</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=strive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>strive</strong></a> to improve, make an effort and attempt new methods to fulfil our responsibilities.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们必须力争进步、付出努力并尝试新方法，以履行责任。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Every attempt requires effort, but teams that <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=strive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>strive</strong></a> together are more likely to fulfil their goals.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">每次尝试都需要努力，但共同奋斗的团队更可能实现目标。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=strive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>strive</strong></a> to fulfil their potential, and each attempt deserves recognition for the effort involved.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生努力发挥潜力，每次尝试都因其中的努力而值得认可。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">strive for excellence</span> · 追求卓越</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perseverance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">perseverance</a></span> · 坚持</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=achievement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">achievement</a></span> · 成就</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-165">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-165" class="vocabulary-word-title">effort<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-165" aria-label="定位 effort">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-165" data-mark-label="effort" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="effort：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="effort：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=effort&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 effort" title="在有道查询 effort">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-165</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.努力；努力的成果</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We must strive to improve, make an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=effort&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>effort</strong></a> and attempt new methods to fulfil our responsibilities.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们必须力争进步、付出努力并尝试新方法，以履行责任。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Every attempt requires <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=effort&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>effort</strong></a>, but teams that strive together are more likely to fulfil their goals.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">每次尝试都需要努力，但共同奋斗的团队更可能实现目标。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students strive to fulfil their potential, and each attempt deserves recognition for the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=effort&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>effort</strong></a> involved.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生努力发挥潜力，每次尝试都因其中的努力而值得认可。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">make an effort</span> · 努力</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perseverance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">perseverance</a></span> · 坚持</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=achievement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">achievement</a></span> · 成就</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-166">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-166" class="vocabulary-word-title">fulfil<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-166" aria-label="定位 fulfil">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-166" data-mark-label="fulfil" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="fulfil：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="fulfil：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fulfil&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 fulfil" title="在有道查询 fulfil">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-166</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.履行，实现；完成；满足</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We must strive to improve, make an effort and attempt new methods to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fulfil&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fulfil</strong></a> our responsibilities.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们必须力争进步、付出努力并尝试新方法，以履行责任。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Every attempt requires effort, but teams that strive together are more likely to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fulfil&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fulfil</strong></a> their goals.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">每次尝试都需要努力，但共同奋斗的团队更可能实现目标。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students strive to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=fulfil&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>fulfil</strong></a> their potential, and each attempt deserves recognition for the effort involved.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生努力发挥潜力，每次尝试都因其中的努力而值得认可。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">fulfil a duty</span> · 履行职责</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=perseverance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">perseverance</a></span> · 坚持</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=achievement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">achievement</a></span> · 成就</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-167">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-167" class="vocabulary-word-title">range<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-167" aria-label="定位 range">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-167" data-mark-label="range" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="range：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="range：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=range&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 range" title="在有道查询 range">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-167</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.(在一定幅度内)变动</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As deadlines loom, we need to sort a wide <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=range&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>range</strong></a> of applications.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">截止日期临近时，我们需整理大量不同申请。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=range&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>range</strong></a> of problems can loom ahead unless teams sort priorities early.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果团队不及早理清优先事项，一系列问题可能迫近。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">When difficulties loom, advisers help students sort through a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=range&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>range</strong></a> of options.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">困难逼近时，顾问帮助学生理清各种选项。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a wide range</span> · 广泛范围</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=priority&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">priority</a></span> · 优先事项</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=planning&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">planning</a></span> · 规划</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-168">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As deadlines loom, we need to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sort&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sort</strong></a> a wide range of applications.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">截止日期临近时，我们需整理大量不同申请。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A range of problems can loom ahead unless teams <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sort&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sort</strong></a> priorities early.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果团队不及早理清优先事项，一系列问题可能迫近。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">When difficulties loom, advisers help students <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sort&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sort</strong></a> through a range of options.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">困难逼近时，顾问帮助学生理清各种选项。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">sort the files</span> · 分类文件</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=priority&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">priority</a></span> · 优先事项</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=planning&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">planning</a></span> · 规划</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-169">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.(庞然大物或形状模糊之物)隐现；逼近；n.织布机</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As deadlines <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=loom&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>loom</strong></a>, we need to sort a wide range of applications.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">截止日期临近时，我们需整理大量不同申请。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A range of problems can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=loom&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>loom</strong></a> ahead unless teams sort priorities early.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果团队不及早理清优先事项，一系列问题可能迫近。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">When difficulties <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=loom&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>loom</strong></a>, advisers help students sort through a range of options.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">困难逼近时，顾问帮助学生理清各种选项。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">loom ahead</span> · 隐约临近</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=priority&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">priority</a></span> · 优先事项</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=planning&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">planning</a></span> · 规划</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-170">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-170" class="vocabulary-word-title">launch<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-170" aria-label="定位 launch">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-170" data-mark-label="launch" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="launch：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="launch：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=launch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 launch" title="在有道查询 launch">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-170</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.发射；发布，推出；发起(运动等)</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before we <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=launch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>launch</strong></a> the project and commence work, we must explore how to exploit resources responsibly.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">启动项目、开始工作前，我们必须探讨如何负责任地利用资源。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers will commence trials, explore new materials, exploit existing data and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=launch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>launch</strong></a> a pilot scheme.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究者将开始试验、探索新材料、利用现有数据，并启动试点项目。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The company plans to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=launch&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>launch</strong></a> a laboratory, commence recruitment, explore alternatives and exploit advances in recycling.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公司计划开设实验室、开始招聘、探索替代方案，并利用回收技术的进展。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">launch a project</span> · 启动项目</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=innovation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">innovation</a></span> · 创新</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pilot%20study&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">pilot study</a></span> · 试点研究</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-171">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-171" class="vocabulary-word-title">commence<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-171" aria-label="定位 commence">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-171" data-mark-label="commence" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="commence：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="commence：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 commence" title="在有道查询 commence">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-171</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v开始，着手</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before we launch the project and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>commence</strong></a> work, we must explore how to exploit resources responsibly.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">启动项目、开始工作前，我们必须探讨如何负责任地利用资源。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>commence</strong></a> trials, explore new materials, exploit existing data and launch a pilot scheme.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究者将开始试验、探索新材料、利用现有数据，并启动试点项目。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The company plans to launch a laboratory, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>commence</strong></a> recruitment, explore alternatives and exploit advances in recycling.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公司计划开设实验室、开始招聘、探索替代方案，并利用回收技术的进展。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">commence work</span> · 开始工作</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=innovation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">innovation</a></span> · 创新</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pilot%20study&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">pilot study</a></span> · 试点研究</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-172">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-172" class="vocabulary-word-title">exploit<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-172" aria-label="定位 exploit">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-172" data-mark-label="exploit" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="exploit：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="exploit：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exploit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 exploit" title="在有道查询 exploit">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-172</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.开发，开采，开拓</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before we launch the project and commence work, we must explore how to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exploit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>exploit</strong></a> resources responsibly.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">启动项目、开始工作前，我们必须探讨如何负责任地利用资源。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers will commence trials, explore new materials, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exploit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>exploit</strong></a> existing data and launch a pilot scheme.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究者将开始试验、探索新材料、利用现有数据，并启动试点项目。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The company plans to launch a laboratory, commence recruitment, explore alternatives and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exploit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>exploit</strong></a> advances in recycling.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公司计划开设实验室、开始招聘、探索替代方案，并利用回收技术的进展。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">exploit resources</span> · 利用资源</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=innovation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">innovation</a></span> · 创新</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pilot%20study&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">pilot study</a></span> · 试点研究</li></ul>
</div>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-173">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-173" class="vocabulary-word-title">explore<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-173" aria-label="定位 explore">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-173" data-mark-label="explore" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="explore：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="explore：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=explore&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 explore" title="在有道查询 explore">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-173</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.勘查，考察；探讨</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before we launch the project and commence work, we must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=explore&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>explore</strong></a> how to exploit resources responsibly.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">启动项目、开始工作前，我们必须探讨如何负责任地利用资源。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers will commence trials, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=explore&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>explore</strong></a> new materials, exploit existing data and launch a pilot scheme.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究者将开始试验、探索新材料、利用现有数据，并启动试点项目。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The company plans to launch a laboratory, commence recruitment, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=explore&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>explore</strong></a> alternatives and exploit advances in recycling.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公司计划开设实验室、开始招聘、探索替代方案，并利用回收技术的进展。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">explore an idea</span> · 探索想法</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=innovation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">innovation</a></span> · 创新</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pilot%20study&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">pilot study</a></span> · 试点研究</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-174">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-174" class="vocabulary-word-title">exert<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-174" aria-label="定位 exert">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-174" data-mark-label="exert" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="exert：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="exert：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exert&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 exert" title="在有道查询 exert">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-174</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.运用，行使；施加</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Teams conduct research, tackle pollution, dispose of waste, cope with shortages and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exert&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>exert</strong></a> pressure for reform.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">团队开展研究、处理污染、处置废物、应对短缺并施压推动改革。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To tackle flooding, we conduct surveys, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exert&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>exert</strong></a> oversight, dispose of debris and help residents cope.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为应对洪灾，我们开展调查、实施监督、处置废墟，并帮助居民应对。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Local agencies must conduct inspections, tackle hazards, dispose of chemicals and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exert&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>exert</strong></a> leadership so communities can cope.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地方机构必须开展检查、处理隐患、处置化学品，并发挥领导作用，让社区能应对。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">exert influence</span> · 施加影响</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=problem%20solving&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">problem solving</a></span> · 解决问题</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=waste%20management&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">waste management</a></span> · 废物管理</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-175">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-175" class="vocabulary-word-title">tackle<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-175" aria-label="定位 tackle">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-175" data-mark-label="tackle" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="tackle：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="tackle：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tackle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 tackle" title="在有道查询 tackle">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-175</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.处理，应对，解决；n.用处，器材</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Teams conduct research, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tackle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tackle</strong></a> pollution, dispose of waste, cope with shortages and exert pressure for reform.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">团队开展研究、处理污染、处置废物、应对短缺并施压推动改革。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tackle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tackle</strong></a> flooding, we conduct surveys, exert oversight, dispose of debris and help residents cope.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为应对洪灾，我们开展调查、实施监督、处置废墟，并帮助居民应对。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Local agencies must conduct inspections, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tackle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>tackle</strong></a> hazards, dispose of chemicals and exert leadership so communities can cope.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地方机构必须开展检查、处理隐患、处置化学品，并发挥领导作用，让社区能应对。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">tackle a problem</span> · 处理问题</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=problem%20solving&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">problem solving</a></span> · 解决问题</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=waste%20management&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">waste management</a></span> · 废物管理</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To tackle flooding, we conduct surveys, exert oversight, dispose of debris and help residents <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cope&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cope</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为应对洪灾，我们开展调查、实施监督、处置废墟，并帮助居民应对。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Local agencies must conduct inspections, tackle hazards, dispose of chemicals and exert leadership so communities can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cope&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cope</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">地方机构必须开展检查、处理隐患、处置化学品，并发挥领导作用，让社区能应对。</p></li>
</ol>

### 词群 20.17｜删除之后怎样恢复与补偿 {#group-20-17}

**串联方式：** 省略、删除、取消和清除之后，再看求助、恢复、纠正、弥补、抵消和补充。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-179">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-179" class="vocabulary-word-title">omit<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-179" aria-label="定位 omit">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-179" data-mark-label="omit" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="omit：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="omit：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=omit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 omit" title="在有道查询 omit">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-179</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.遗漏；省略；删去</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you cancel the account, delete old files, erase recordings, clear shared folders and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=omit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>omit</strong></a> personal details from the final report.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">注销账户前，删除旧文件、抹除录音、清空共享文件夹，并从最终报告中省去个人信息。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The guide explains how to cancel a form, delete an entry, erase a note, clear a field and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=omit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>omit</strong></a> an optional answer.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">指南解释如何取消表格、删除条目、抹掉备注、清空字段及省略可选答案。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Please do not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=omit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>omit</strong></a> the warning: if you cancel this operation, it may still delete files, erase notes and clear the archive.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">请勿省略警告：即使取消这项操作，它仍可能删除文件、抹去备注并清空档案。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">omit a detail</span> · 省略细节</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=data%20management&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">data management</a></span> · 数据管理</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=privacy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">privacy</a></span> · 隐私</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-180">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-180" class="vocabulary-word-title">delete<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-180" aria-label="定位 delete">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-180" data-mark-label="delete" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="delete：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="delete：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=delete&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 delete" title="在有道查询 delete">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-180</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.删除，删掉</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you cancel the account, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=delete&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>delete</strong></a> old files, erase recordings, clear shared folders and omit personal details from the final report.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">注销账户前，删除旧文件、抹除录音、清空共享文件夹，并从最终报告中省去个人信息。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The guide explains how to cancel a form, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=delete&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>delete</strong></a> an entry, erase a note, clear a field and omit an optional answer.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">指南解释如何取消表格、删除条目、抹掉备注、清空字段及省略可选答案。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Please do not omit the warning: if you cancel this operation, it may still <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=delete&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>delete</strong></a> files, erase notes and clear the archive.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">请勿省略警告：即使取消这项操作，它仍可能删除文件、抹去备注并清空档案。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">delete a file</span> · 删除文件</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=data%20management&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">data management</a></span> · 数据管理</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=privacy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">privacy</a></span> · 隐私</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-184">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-184" class="vocabulary-word-title">resort<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-184" aria-label="定位 resort">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-184" data-mark-label="resort" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="resort：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="resort：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=resort&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 resort" title="在有道查询 resort">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-184</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.求助于，采取，诉诸；n.(度假)胜地</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To recover the files and restore access, technicians will rectify the error before they <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=resort&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>resort</strong></a> to a full reset.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为恢复文件和访问权限，技术人员会先纠错，再考虑彻底重置。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We hope to recover the funds, rectify the accounts and restore trust without having to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=resort&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>resort</strong></a> to legal action.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们希望追回资金、纠正账目并恢复信任，而不必诉诸法律。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A backup helps us recover information, rectify mistakes and restore normal service before we <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=resort&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>resort</strong></a> to manual records.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">备份帮助我们恢复信息、纠正错误并恢复正常服务，而不必诉诸手写记录。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">resort to borrowing</span> · 诉诸借款</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=repair&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">repair</a></span> · 修复</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=backup%20copy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">backup copy</a></span> · 备份副本</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-185">
