<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.重新拿回；恢复健康；(从不愉快的经历申)复原</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recover&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>recover</strong></a> the files and restore access, technicians will rectify the error before they resort to a full reset.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为恢复文件和访问权限，技术人员会先纠错，再考虑彻底重置。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We hope to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recover&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>recover</strong></a> the funds, rectify the accounts and restore trust without having to resort to legal action.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们希望追回资金、纠正账目并恢复信任，而不必诉诸法律。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A backup helps us <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recover&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>recover</strong></a> information, rectify mistakes and restore normal service before we resort to manual records.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">备份帮助我们恢复信息、纠正错误并恢复正常服务，而不必诉诸手写记录。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">recover lost data</span> · 恢复丢失数据</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=repair&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">repair</a></span> · 修复</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=backup%20copy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">backup copy</a></span> · 备份副本</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To recover the files and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=restore&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>restore</strong></a> access, technicians will rectify the error before they resort to a full reset.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为恢复文件和访问权限，技术人员会先纠错，再考虑彻底重置。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We hope to recover the funds, rectify the accounts and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=restore&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>restore</strong></a> trust without having to resort to legal action.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们希望追回资金、纠正账目并恢复信任，而不必诉诸法律。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A backup helps us recover information, rectify mistakes and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=restore&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>restore</strong></a> normal service before we resort to manual records.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">备份帮助我们恢复信息、纠正错误并恢复正常服务，而不必诉诸手写记录。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">restore a building</span> · 修复建筑</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-187">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v纠正，矫正，改正</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To recover the files and restore access, technicians will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rectify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rectify</strong></a> the error before they resort to a full reset.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为恢复文件和访问权限，技术人员会先纠错，再考虑彻底重置。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We hope to recover the funds, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rectify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rectify</strong></a> the accounts and restore trust without having to resort to legal action.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们希望追回资金、纠正账目并恢复信任，而不必诉诸法律。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A backup helps us recover information, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=rectify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>rectify</strong></a> mistakes and restore normal service before we resort to manual records.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">备份帮助我们恢复信息、纠正错误并恢复正常服务，而不必诉诸手写记录。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">rectify an error</span> · 纠正错误</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=repair&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">repair</a></span> · 修复</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=backup%20copy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">backup copy</a></span> · 备份副本</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-188">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-188" class="vocabulary-word-title">redeem<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-188" aria-label="定位 redeem">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-188" data-mark-label="redeem" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="redeem：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="redeem：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=redeem&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 redeem" title="在有道查询 redeem">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-188</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.赎回；挽回，补偿；兑现</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Customers can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=redeem&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>redeem</strong></a> a voucher to replenish supplies and offset some of the cost.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">顾客可兑换代金券来补充物资，并抵消部分费用。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The charity will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=redeem&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>redeem</strong></a> donated vouchers, replenish food stocks and offset a funding shortfall.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">慈善机构将兑换捐赠的代金券、补充食品库存，并弥补资金缺口。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To offset the expense of travel, students may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=redeem&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>redeem</strong></a> credits and use the saving to replenish their study materials.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为抵消交通费用，学生可兑换积分，并用节余补充学习资料。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">redeem a voucher</span> · 兑换代金券</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=restocking&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">restocking</a></span> · 补货</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=credit&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">credit</a></span> · 积分</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Customers can redeem a voucher to replenish supplies and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=offset&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>offset</strong></a> some of the cost.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">顾客可兑换代金券来补充物资，并抵消部分费用。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The charity will redeem donated vouchers, replenish food stocks and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=offset&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>offset</strong></a> a funding shortfall.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">慈善机构将兑换捐赠的代金券、补充食品库存，并弥补资金缺口。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=offset&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>offset</strong></a> the expense of travel, students may redeem credits and use the saving to replenish their study materials.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为抵消交通费用，学生可兑换积分，并用节余补充学习资料。</p></li>

### 词群 20.18｜获得、提供与增强 {#group-20-18}

**串联方式：** 由取得和提供进入扩大、放大、夸张、延长与支持，再用推动、更新和提高描述变化。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-191">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-191" class="vocabulary-word-title">obtain<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-191" aria-label="定位 obtain">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-191" data-mark-label="obtain" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="obtain：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="obtain：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=obtain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 obtain" title="在有道查询 obtain">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-191</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v获得，得到</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Volunteers gain experience, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=obtain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>obtain</strong></a> training and offer their time while donors supply equipment and render assistance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">志愿者获得经验、接受培训并奉献时间，捐助者则提供设备及协助。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=obtain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>obtain</strong></a> approval and gain trust, the agency must supply evidence, offer clear advice and render an honest account.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为获批并赢得信任，机构须提供证据、提出明确建议，并如实说明情况。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Partners offer expertise, supply materials and render technical help so schools can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=obtain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>obtain</strong></a> resources and gain independence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">伙伴提供专长、材料及技术帮助，让学校获得资源并实现独立。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">obtain permission</span> · 获得许可</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=provision&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">provision</a></span> · 提供</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assistance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">assistance</a></span> · 援助</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-192">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-192" class="vocabulary-word-title">gain<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-192" aria-label="定位 gain">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-192" data-mark-label="gain" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="gain：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="gain：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 gain" title="在有道查询 gain">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-192</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.获得；增加；n.增加；改造</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Volunteers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gain</strong></a> experience, obtain training and offer their time while donors supply equipment and render assistance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">志愿者获得经验、接受培训并奉献时间，捐助者则提供设备及协助。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To obtain approval and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gain</strong></a> trust, the agency must supply evidence, offer clear advice and render an honest account.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为获批并赢得信任，机构须提供证据、提出明确建议，并如实说明情况。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Partners offer expertise, supply materials and render technical help so schools can obtain resources and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gain</strong></a> independence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">伙伴提供专长、材料及技术帮助，让学校获得资源并实现独立。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">gain experience</span> · 获得经验</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=provision&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">provision</a></span> · 提供</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assistance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">assistance</a></span> · 援助</li></ul>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Volunteers gain experience, obtain training and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=offer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>offer</strong></a> their time while donors supply equipment and render assistance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">志愿者获得经验、接受培训并奉献时间，捐助者则提供设备及协助。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To obtain approval and gain trust, the agency must supply evidence, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=offer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>offer</strong></a> clear advice and render an honest account.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为获批并赢得信任，机构须提供证据、提出明确建议，并如实说明情况。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Partners <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=offer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>offer</strong></a> expertise, supply materials and render technical help so schools can obtain resources and gain independence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">伙伴提供专长、材料及技术帮助，让学校获得资源并实现独立。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">offer support</span> · 提供支持</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=provision&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">provision</a></span> · 提供</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assistance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">assistance</a></span> · 援助</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-195">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-195" class="vocabulary-word-title">render<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-195" aria-label="定位 render">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-195" data-mark-label="render" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="render：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="render：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=render&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 render" title="在有道查询 render">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-195</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.给予，提供；翻译</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Volunteers gain experience, obtain training and offer their time while donors supply equipment and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=render&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>render</strong></a> assistance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">志愿者获得经验、接受培训并奉献时间，捐助者则提供设备及协助。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">To obtain approval and gain trust, the agency must supply evidence, offer clear advice and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=render&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>render</strong></a> an honest account.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为获批并赢得信任，机构须提供证据、提出明确建议，并如实说明情况。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Partners offer expertise, supply materials and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=render&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>render</strong></a> technical help so schools can obtain resources and gain independence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">伙伴提供专长、材料及技术帮助，让学校获得资源并实现独立。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">render assistance</span> · 给予协助</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=provision&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">provision</a></span> · 提供</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assistance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">assistance</a></span> · 援助</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-196">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-196" class="vocabulary-word-title">enlarge<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-196" aria-label="定位 enlarge">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-196" data-mark-label="enlarge" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="enlarge：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="enlarge：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enlarge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 enlarge" title="在有道查询 enlarge">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-196</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.扩大，扩展；放大(照片或文件）</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Digital tools can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enlarge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>enlarge</strong></a> an image, magnify detail, amplify sound and augment a presentation, but should not exaggerate the evidence.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">数字工具能放大图像与细节、增强声音并丰富展示，但不应夸大证据。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Editors may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enlarge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>enlarge</strong></a> photos, magnify defects and amplify criticism to augment interest, yet this can exaggerate a minor problem.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">编辑可能放大照片与缺陷、强化批评来增加关注，但这可能夸大小问题。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We use technology to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enlarge&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>enlarge</strong></a> diagrams, magnify small features, amplify quiet recordings and augment learning without trying to exaggerate results.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们用技术放大图解与细小特征、增强轻声录音并充实学习，而不夸大成果。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">enlarge an image</span> · 放大图像</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=enhancement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">enhancement</a></span> · 增强</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=distortion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">distortion</a></span> · 扭曲</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Editors may enlarge photos, magnify defects and amplify criticism to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=augment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>augment</strong></a> interest, yet this can exaggerate a minor problem.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">编辑可能放大照片与缺陷、强化批评来增加关注，但这可能夸大小问题。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We use technology to enlarge diagrams, magnify small features, amplify quiet recordings and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=augment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>augment</strong></a> learning without trying to exaggerate results.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们用技术放大图解与细小特征、增强轻声录音并充实学习，而不夸大成果。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Editors may enlarge photos, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=magnify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>magnify</strong></a> defects and amplify criticism to augment interest, yet this can exaggerate a minor problem.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">编辑可能放大照片与缺陷、强化批评来增加关注，但这可能夸大小问题。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Editors may enlarge photos, magnify defects and amplify criticism to augment interest, yet this can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exaggerate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>exaggerate</strong></a> a minor problem.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">编辑可能放大照片与缺陷、强化批评来增加关注，但这可能夸大小问题。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We must update training to raise standards and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=propel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>propel</strong></a> progress.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们必须更新培训，以提高标准并推动进步。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An update to the software could raise efficiency and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=propel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>propel</strong></a> the project forward.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">软件更新可以提高效率并推动项目进展。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Schools that update their curriculum can raise expectations and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=propel&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>propel</strong></a> students towards new opportunities.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">更新课程的学校能提高期待，推动学生走向新机遇。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">propel growth</span> · 推动增长</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=improvement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">improvement</a></span> · 改进</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=development&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">development</a></span> · 发展</li></ul>

### 词群 20.19｜容纳、渗漏与排水场景 {#group-20-19}

**串联方式：** 用容纳、泄漏、溢出、渗透和饱和描述物质，再联系撤离与挖沟；trench 是沟渠或挖沟，不是 drench 湿透。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-207">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-207" class="vocabulary-word-title">contain<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-207" aria-label="定位 contain">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-207" data-mark-label="contain" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="contain：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="contain：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 contain" title="在有道查询 contain">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-207</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.包含，容纳；抑制(感情)</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The container must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>contain</strong></a> the liquid without a leak or spill.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">容器必须装住液体，不能泄漏或洒出。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A tray can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>contain</strong></a> a small spill while staff locate the leak.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工作人员寻找漏点时，托盘能容纳少量泄漏液。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not overfill the tank: it may leak, spill or fail to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=contain&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>contain</strong></a> the pressure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要把罐装得过满：它可能泄漏、溢出，或无法承受压力。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">contain a liquid</span> · 容纳液体</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=containment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">containment</a></span> · 密封容纳</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spillage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">spillage</a></span> · 溢洒</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-208">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-208" class="vocabulary-word-title">leak<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-208" aria-label="定位 leak">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-208" data-mark-label="leak" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="leak：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="leak：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=leak&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 leak" title="在有道查询 leak">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-208</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.泄漏；n.漏洞,裂缝</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The container must contain the liquid without a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=leak&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>leak</strong></a> or spill.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">容器必须装住液体，不能泄漏或洒出。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A tray can contain a small spill while staff locate the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=leak&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>leak</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工作人员寻找漏点时，托盘能容纳少量泄漏液。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not overfill the tank: it may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=leak&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>leak</strong></a>, spill or fail to contain the pressure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要把罐装得过满：它可能泄漏、溢出，或无法承受压力。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a gas leak</span> · 燃气泄漏</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=containment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">containment</a></span> · 密封容纳</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spillage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">spillage</a></span> · 溢洒</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-209">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.(使)溢出</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The container must contain the liquid without a leak or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spill&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spill</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">容器必须装住液体，不能泄漏或洒出。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A tray can contain a small <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spill&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spill</strong></a> while staff locate the leak.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">工作人员寻找漏点时，托盘能容纳少量泄漏液。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Do not overfill the tank: it may leak, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spill&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spill</strong></a> or fail to contain the pressure.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">不要把罐装得过满：它可能泄漏、溢出，或无法承受压力。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">spill a drink</span> · 洒出饮料</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=containment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">containment</a></span> · 密封容纳</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spillage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">spillage</a></span> · 溢洒</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-210">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-210" class="vocabulary-word-title">seep<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-210" aria-label="定位 seep">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-210" data-mark-label="seep" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="seep：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="seep：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=seep&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 seep" title="在有道查询 seep">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-210</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.渗出，渗漏</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Water can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=seep&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>seep</strong></a> through a trench, ooze from cracks and saturate the ground, forcing officials to evacuate residents.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">水可能渗过沟渠、从裂缝渗出并浸透地面，迫使官员疏散居民。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Inspectors watched water <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=seep&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>seep</strong></a> into the trench and mud ooze out as rain began to saturate the slope, prompting plans to evacuate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">检查员看着水渗入沟渠、泥浆涌出，同时雨水开始浸透山坡，促使他们规划撤离。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We may need to evacuate the site if water continues to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=seep&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>seep</strong></a>, saturate the soil and ooze into the drainage trench.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果水继续渗漏、浸透土壤并流进排水沟，我们可能需要撤离现场。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">seep through soil</span> · 渗透土壤</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drainage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">drainage</a></span> · 排水</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flood%20risk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">flood risk</a></span> · 洪灾风险</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-211">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-211" class="vocabulary-word-title">ooze<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-211" aria-label="定位 ooze">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-211" data-mark-label="ooze" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="ooze：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="ooze：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ooze&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 ooze" title="在有道查询 ooze">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-211</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.渗出；洋溢着</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Water can seep through a trench, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ooze&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ooze</strong></a> from cracks and saturate the ground, forcing officials to evacuate residents.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">水可能渗过沟渠、从裂缝渗出并浸透地面，迫使官员疏散居民。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Inspectors watched water seep into the trench and mud <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ooze&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ooze</strong></a> out as rain began to saturate the slope, prompting plans to evacuate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">检查员看着水渗入沟渠、泥浆涌出，同时雨水开始浸透山坡，促使他们规划撤离。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We may need to evacuate the site if water continues to seep, saturate the soil and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ooze&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>ooze</strong></a> into the drainage trench.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果水继续渗漏、浸透土壤并流进排水沟，我们可能需要撤离现场。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">ooze from a crack</span> · 从裂缝渗出</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drainage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">drainage</a></span> · 排水</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=flood%20risk&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">flood risk</a></span> · 洪灾风险</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-212">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-212" class="vocabulary-word-title">evacuate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-212" aria-label="定位 evacuate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-212" data-mark-label="evacuate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="evacuate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="evacuate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=evacuate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 evacuate" title="在有道查询 evacuate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-212</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.疏散，撤离</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Water can seep through a trench, ooze from cracks and saturate the ground, forcing officials to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=evacuate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>evacuate</strong></a> residents.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">水可能渗过沟渠、从裂缝渗出并浸透地面，迫使官员疏散居民。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Inspectors watched water seep into the trench and mud ooze out as rain began to saturate the slope, prompting plans to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=evacuate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>evacuate</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">检查员看着水渗入沟渠、泥浆涌出，同时雨水开始浸透山坡，促使他们规划撤离。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We may need to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=evacuate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>evacuate</strong></a> the site if water continues to seep, saturate the soil and ooze into the drainage trench.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果水继续渗漏、浸透土壤并流进排水沟，我们可能需要撤离现场。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">evacuate residents</span> · 疏散居民</p>

### 词群 20.20｜人际回应与态度 {#group-20-20}

**串联方式：** 比较原谅、看待、奉承和崇拜；regard 常需与 as 连用说明如何看待。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-215">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-215" class="vocabulary-word-title">forgive<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-215" aria-label="定位 forgive">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-215" data-mark-label="forgive" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="forgive：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="forgive：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=forgive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 forgive" title="在有道查询 forgive">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-215</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.原谅，宽恕，饶恕</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">We can regard leaders with respect and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=forgive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>forgive</strong></a> mistakes without feeling obliged to flatter or worship them.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们可以尊重领导者并原谅错误，而不必奉承或崇拜他们。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The essay asks why people worship celebrities, flatter them, regard them as perfect and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=forgive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>forgive</strong></a> serious misconduct.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">文章探讨人们为何崇拜名人、奉承他们、认为他们完美，并原谅严重不当行为。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">True friends regard honesty as essential; they <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=forgive&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>forgive</strong></a> errors but do not flatter each other or worship success.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">真朋友认为诚实不可或缺；他们原谅错误，却不互相奉承或崇拜成功。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">forgive a mistake</span> · 原谅错误</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=respect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">respect</a></span> · 尊重</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=admiration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">admiration</a></span> · 钦佩</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">True friends <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=regard&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>regard</strong></a> honesty as essential; they forgive errors but do not flatter each other or worship success.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">真朋友认为诚实不可或缺；他们原谅错误，却不互相奉承或崇拜成功。</p></li>
</ol>

### 词群 20.21｜说明原因、目的与触发 {#group-20-21}

**串联方式：** 先说明原因、缘故、目的和目标，再用唤起、触发描述事情怎样启动。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-219">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-219" class="vocabulary-word-title">cause<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-219" aria-label="定位 cause">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-219" data-mark-label="cause" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="cause：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="cause：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cause&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 cause" title="在有道查询 cause">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-219</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n原因,理由；事业</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For the sake of clarity, explain the purpose and objective of the policy and the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cause&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cause</strong></a> of the problem.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为清楚起见，请解释政策的目的与目标，以及问题的成因。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A project needs a clear purpose and objective rather than change for its own sake that may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cause&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cause</strong></a> confusion.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">项目需要明确目的与目标，而非为了改变而改变，导致混乱。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Our objective is to identify the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cause&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cause</strong></a> of pollution for the sake of public health, which is the purpose of this research.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们的目标是为公众健康找出污染成因，这也是研究的目的。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">cause a problem</span> · 引发问题</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=motivation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">motivation</a></span> · 动机</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reason&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reason</a></span> · 原因</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-220">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-220" class="vocabulary-word-title">sake<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-220" aria-label="定位 sake">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-220" data-mark-label="sake" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="sake：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="sake：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 sake" title="在有道查询 sake">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-220</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.理由，目的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sake</strong></a> of clarity, explain the purpose and objective of the policy and the cause of the problem.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为清楚起见，请解释政策的目的与目标，以及问题的成因。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A project needs a clear purpose and objective rather than change for its own <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sake</strong></a> that may cause confusion.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">项目需要明确目的与目标，而非为了改变而改变，导致混乱。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Our objective is to identify the cause of pollution for the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sake&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sake</strong></a> of public health, which is the purpose of this research.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们的目标是为公众健康找出污染成因，这也是研究的目的。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">for the sake of</span> · 为了</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=motivation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">motivation</a></span> · 动机</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reason&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reason</a></span> · 原因</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-222">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-222" class="vocabulary-word-title">objective<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-222" aria-label="定位 objective">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-222" data-mark-label="objective" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="objective：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="objective：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=objective&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 objective" title="在有道查询 objective">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-222</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.宗旨；目标；adj.客观的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">For the sake of clarity, explain the purpose and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=objective&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>objective</strong></a> of the policy and the cause of the problem.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">为清楚起见，请解释政策的目的与目标，以及问题的成因。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A project needs a clear purpose and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=objective&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>objective</strong></a> rather than change for its own sake that may cause confusion.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">项目需要明确目的与目标，而非为了改变而改变，导致混乱。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Our <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=objective&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>objective</strong></a> is to identify the cause of pollution for the sake of public health, which is the purpose of this research.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我们的目标是为公众健康找出污染成因，这也是研究的目的。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a policy objective</span> · 政策目标</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=motivation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">motivation</a></span> · 动机</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reason&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reason</a></span> · 原因</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-223">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-223" class="vocabulary-word-title">arouse<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-223" aria-label="定位 arouse">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-223" data-mark-label="arouse" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="arouse：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="arouse：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arouse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 arouse" title="在有道查询 arouse">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-223</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v唤起，激起，引起</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A striking image can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arouse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>arouse</strong></a> interest and trigger discussion.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">醒目图像能引起兴趣并引发讨论。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The announcement may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arouse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>arouse</strong></a> concern and trigger a formal review.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公告可能引起担忧并触发正式审查。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Teachers use unexpected questions to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=arouse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>arouse</strong></a> curiosity and trigger deeper thinking.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">老师用意想不到的问题激起好奇心，引发深入思考。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">arouse interest</span> · 引起兴趣</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=response&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">response</a></span> · 回应</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=stimulus&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">stimulus</a></span> · 刺激</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-224">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A striking image can arouse interest and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trigger&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trigger</strong></a> discussion.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">醒目图像能引起兴趣并引发讨论。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The announcement may arouse concern and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trigger&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trigger</strong></a> a formal review.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">公告可能引起担忧并触发正式审查。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Teachers use unexpected questions to arouse curiosity and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trigger&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>trigger</strong></a> deeper thinking.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">老师用意想不到的问题激起好奇心，引发深入思考。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">trigger a reaction</span> · 引发反应</p>

### 词群 20.22｜寻找、求证与传授 {#group-20-22}

**串联方式：** 找到并取回资料之后，作证、核验、明确说明，再启发和传授；deserve 用于应得。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-225">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-225" class="vocabulary-word-title">seek<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-225" aria-label="定位 seek">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-225" data-mark-label="seek" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="seek：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="seek：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=seek&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 seek" title="在有道查询 seek">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-225</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.寻找；寻求</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Investigators <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=seek&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>seek</strong></a> records, retrieve files and verify claims before asking a witness to testify.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查人员寻找记录、取回文件并核实主张，再请证人作证。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before I testify, I must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=seek&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>seek</strong></a> permission to retrieve the documents and verify their dates.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">作证前，我必须申请许可取回文件，并核实日期。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The team will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=seek&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>seek</strong></a> a witness who can retrieve the messages, verify their origin and testify about the exchange.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">团队将寻找能找回消息、核实来源，并就交流过程作证的证人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">seek evidence</span> · 寻找证据</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=verification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">verification</a></span> · 核实</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=testimony&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">testimony</a></span> · 证词</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-226">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-226" class="vocabulary-word-title">retrieve<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-226" aria-label="定位 retrieve">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-226" data-mark-label="retrieve" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="retrieve：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="retrieve：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retrieve&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 retrieve" title="在有道查询 retrieve">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-226</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.取回；挽回</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Investigators seek records, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retrieve&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>retrieve</strong></a> files and verify claims before asking a witness to testify.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查人员寻找记录、取回文件并核实主张，再请证人作证。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before I testify, I must seek permission to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retrieve&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>retrieve</strong></a> the documents and verify their dates.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">作证前，我必须申请许可取回文件，并核实日期。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The team will seek a witness who can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retrieve&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>retrieve</strong></a> the messages, verify their origin and testify about the exchange.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">团队将寻找能找回消息、核实来源，并就交流过程作证的证人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">retrieve a file</span> · 取回文件</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=verification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">verification</a></span> · 核实</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=testimony&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">testimony</a></span> · 证词</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-227">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-227" class="vocabulary-word-title">testify<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-227" aria-label="定位 testify">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-227" data-mark-label="testify" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="testify：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="testify：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=testify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 testify" title="在有道查询 testify">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-227</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.作证；证明</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Investigators seek records, retrieve files and verify claims before asking a witness to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=testify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>testify</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">调查人员寻找记录、取回文件并核实主张，再请证人作证。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before I <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=testify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>testify</strong></a>, I must seek permission to retrieve the documents and verify their dates.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">作证前，我必须申请许可取回文件，并核实日期。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The team will seek a witness who can retrieve the messages, verify their origin and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=testify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>testify</strong></a> about the exchange.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">团队将寻找能找回消息、核实来源，并就交流过程作证的证人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">testify in court</span> · 出庭作证</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=verification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">verification</a></span> · 核实</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=testimony&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">testimony</a></span> · 证词</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-228">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-228" class="vocabulary-word-title">verify<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-228" aria-label="定位 verify">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-228" data-mark-label="verify" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="verify：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="verify：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=verify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 verify" title="在有道查询 verify">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-228</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before I testify, I must seek permission to retrieve the documents and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=verify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>verify</strong></a> their dates.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">作证前，我必须申请许可取回文件，并核实日期。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The team will seek a witness who can retrieve the messages, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=verify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>verify</strong></a> their origin and testify about the exchange.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">团队将寻找能找回消息、核实来源，并就交流过程作证的证人。</p></li>
</ol>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-229">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-229" class="vocabulary-word-title">specify<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-229" aria-label="定位 specify">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-229" data-mark-label="specify" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="specify：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="specify：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=specify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 specify" title="在有道查询 specify">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-229</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.具体指定，详细说明</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">

### 词群 20.23｜下降与被遮蔽 {#group-20-23}

**串联方式：** 比较一般下降、下沉和暴跌，再辨声音被压低、事物被掩盖的描述。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-233">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-233" class="vocabulary-word-title">drop<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-233" aria-label="定位 drop">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-233" data-mark-label="drop" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="drop：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="drop：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drop&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 drop" title="在有道查询 drop">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-233</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. (使)落下；(使)降低；和…...断绝联系；n.滴</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drop&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>drop</strong></a> in demand can make prices sink and profits plummet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">需求下降可能使价格下跌、利润暴跌。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Analysts watched confidence <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drop&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>drop</strong></a>, investment sink and share prices plummet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">分析人士看着信心下降、投资减少和股价暴跌。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If temperatures <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=drop&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>drop</strong></a>, the mist may sink into the valley as visibility begins to plummet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">气温下降时，雾可能沉入山谷，能见度开始骤降。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a sudden drop</span> · 突然下降</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=downturn&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">downturn</a></span> · 下降</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=decline&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">decline</a></span> · 减少</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-234">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-234" class="vocabulary-word-title">sink<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-234" aria-label="定位 sink">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-234" data-mark-label="sink" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="sink：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="sink：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sink&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 sink" title="在有道查询 sink">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-234</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A drop in demand can make prices <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sink&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sink</strong></a> and profits plummet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">需求下降可能使价格下跌、利润暴跌。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Analysts watched confidence drop, investment <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sink&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sink</strong></a> and share prices plummet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">分析人士看着信心下降、投资减少和股价暴跌。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If temperatures drop, the mist may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=sink&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>sink</strong></a> into the valley as visibility begins to plummet.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">气温下降时，雾可能沉入山谷，能见度开始骤降。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">sink below the surface</span> · 沉入水面下</p>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Thick curtains <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=muffle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>muffle</strong></a> the traffic noise that might otherwise overshadow the quiet performance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">厚窗帘减弱了可能盖过安静演出的交通声。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The editor tried to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=muffle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>muffle</strong></a> criticism, but the scandal continued to overshadow the award.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">编辑试图压低批评声音，但丑闻仍使奖项黯然失色。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Please do not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=muffle&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>muffle</strong></a> quieter voices or let one speaker overshadow the discussion.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">请不要压制较轻的声音，也不要让一位发言者压过整场讨论。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=suppression&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">suppression</a></span> · 压制</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prominence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">prominence</a></span> · 显著性</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 20.24｜从邀请到告别的社交流程 {#group-20-24}

**串联方式：** 邀请、欢迎、问候和致意之后，庆祝、祝贺、祝福与参与，最后告别。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-238">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-238" class="vocabulary-word-title">invite<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-238" aria-label="定位 invite">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-238" data-mark-label="invite" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="invite：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="invite：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=invite&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 invite" title="在有道查询 invite">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-238</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 邀请</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The organisers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=invite&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>invite</strong></a> residents to welcome newcomers, greet neighbours and salute the flag at the ceremony.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">组织者邀请居民欢迎新来者、问候邻居，并在典礼上向旗帜敬礼。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At the reception, hosts welcome visitors, greet each family, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=invite&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>invite</strong></a> them inside and salute the volunteers' dedication.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">招待会上，主人欢迎访客、问候每个家庭、邀请他们进屋，并向志愿者的奉献致敬。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Schools can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=invite&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>invite</strong></a> veterans to greet pupils, welcome questions and salute the achievements of the next generation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学校可邀请老兵问候学生、欢迎提问，并向下一代的成就致敬。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">invite a guest</span> · 邀请客人</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hospitality&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hospitality</a></span> · 好客</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=ceremony&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">ceremony</a></span> · 典礼</li></ul>

</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-242">
