<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v. 庆祝(节日、事件等)</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Friends participate in a farewell party to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=celebrate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>celebrate</strong></a> a new beginning, congratulate the couple and bless their marriage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">朋友参加欢送会，庆祝新开始、祝贺伴侣并祝福他们的婚姻。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At the farewell gathering, guests <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=celebrate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>celebrate</strong></a> the teacher's career, congratulate her on retirement and participate as the minister offers to bless her future.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">欢送会上，宾客庆祝老师的职业成就、祝贺她退休，并参与牧师为其未来祝福的环节。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Those who participate in the farewell ceremony can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=celebrate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>celebrate</strong></a> the graduates, congratulate their families and hear an elder bless the journey ahead.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">参加告别典礼的人能庆祝毕业、祝贺家人，并听长者为前程祝福。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">celebrate success</span> · 庆祝成功</p>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Friends participate in a farewell party to celebrate a new beginning, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=congratulate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>congratulate</strong></a> the couple and bless their marriage.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">朋友参加欢送会，庆祝新开始、祝贺伴侣并祝福他们的婚姻。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At the farewell gathering, guests celebrate the teacher's career, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=congratulate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>congratulate</strong></a> her on retirement and participate as the minister offers to bless her future.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">欢送会上，宾客庆祝老师的职业成就、祝贺她退休，并参与牧师为其未来祝福的环节。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Those who participate in the farewell ceremony can celebrate the graduates, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=congratulate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>congratulate</strong></a> their families and hear an elder bless the journey ahead.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">参加告别典礼的人能庆祝毕业、祝贺家人，并听长者为前程祝福。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=celebration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">celebration</a></span> · 庆祝</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=good%20wishes&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">good wishes</a></span> · 祝愿</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-247">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-247" class="vocabulary-word-title">assort<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-247" aria-label="定位 assort">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-247" data-mark-label="assort" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="assort：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="assort：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assort&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 assort" title="在有道查询 assort">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-247</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.把......分类；相配，协调</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The curator must attribute each painting accurately, ensure its label and records correspond, and check that its frame and colours <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assort&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>assort</strong></a> well, in accord with the exhibition plan.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">策展人须准确认定各画作者、确保标签与记录对应，并依展览计划检查画框与色彩是否协调。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Choices that <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assort&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>assort</strong></a> poorly with the setting may not correspond to public expectations or accord with values we attribute to the institution.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与环境不协调的选择可能不符合公众期待，也不符合我们赋予机构的价值。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The curator will attribute each item to an artist, check that the labels correspond to the records and ensure the frames <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=assort&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>assort</strong></a> well with the room in accord with the plan.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">策展人将认定每件物品的作者、检查标签与记录对应，并依计划确保画框与房间协调。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">assort well with</span> · 与……协调</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=classification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">classification</a></span> · 分类</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consistency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">consistency</a></span> · 一致性</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-248">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-248" class="vocabulary-word-title">correspond<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-248" aria-label="定位 correspond">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-248" data-mark-label="correspond" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="correspond：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="correspond：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=correspond&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 correspond" title="在有道查询 correspond">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-248</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.符合，相一致；通信</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The curator must attribute each painting accurately, ensure its label and records <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=correspond&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>correspond</strong></a>, and check that its frame and colours assort well, in accord with the exhibition plan.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">策展人须准确认定各画作者、确保标签与记录对应，并依展览计划检查画框与色彩是否协调。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Choices that assort poorly with the setting may not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=correspond&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>correspond</strong></a> to public expectations or accord with values we attribute to the institution.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与环境不协调的选择可能不符合公众期待，也不符合我们赋予机构的价值。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The curator will attribute each item to an artist, check that the labels <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=correspond&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>correspond</strong></a> to the records and ensure the frames assort well with the room in accord with the plan.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">策展人将认定每件物品的作者、检查标签与记录对应，并依计划确保画框与房间协调。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">correspond to a category</span> · 对应类别</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=classification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">classification</a></span> · 分类</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consistency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">consistency</a></span> · 一致性</li></ul>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Choices that assort poorly with the setting may not correspond to public expectations or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accord&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>accord</strong></a> with values we attribute to the institution.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与环境不协调的选择可能不符合公众期待，也不符合我们赋予机构的价值。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The curator will attribute each item to an artist, check that the labels correspond to the records and ensure the frames assort well with the room in <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=accord&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>accord</strong></a> with the plan.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">策展人将认定每件物品的作者、检查标签与记录对应，并依计划确保画框与房间协调。</p></li>

</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-250">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-250" class="vocabulary-word-title">attribute<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-250" aria-label="定位 attribute">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-250" data-mark-label="attribute" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="attribute：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="attribute：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attribute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 attribute" title="在有道查询 attribute">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-250</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.把……归因于；n.属性，特性</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The curator must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attribute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>attribute</strong></a> each painting accurately, ensure its label and records correspond, and check that its frame and colours assort well, in accord with the exhibition plan.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">策展人须准确认定各画作者、确保标签与记录对应，并依展览计划检查画框与色彩是否协调。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Choices that assort poorly with the setting may not correspond to public expectations or accord with values we <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attribute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>attribute</strong></a> to the institution.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与环境不协调的选择可能不符合公众期待，也不符合我们赋予机构的价值。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The curator will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attribute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>attribute</strong></a> each item to an artist, check that the labels correspond to the records and ensure the frames assort well with the room in accord with the plan.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">策展人将认定每件物品的作者、检查标签与记录对应，并依计划确保画框与房间协调。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">attribute success to effort</span> · 将成功归因于努力</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=classification&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">classification</a></span> · 分类</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consistency&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">consistency</a></span> · 一致性</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-251">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-251" class="vocabulary-word-title">recall<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-251" aria-label="定位 recall">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-251" data-mark-label="recall" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="recall：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="recall：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recall&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 recall" title="在有道查询 recall">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-251</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.回忆，回想起</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Old photographs remind me of places I can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recall&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>recall</strong></a> but whose names I cannot recollect.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">老照片让我想起记得样貌却想不起名字的地方。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A familiar song can remind us of a journey and help us <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recall&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>recall</strong></a> details we could not otherwise recollect.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">熟悉歌曲能让我们想起旅程，并帮助记起原本想不起的细节。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The smell may remind you of childhood, helping you <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recall&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>recall</strong></a> a kitchen and recollect who cooked there.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">气味可能使你想起童年，帮助回忆厨房及在那里做饭的人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">recall an event</span> · 回忆事件</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=memory&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">memory</a></span> · 记忆</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=association&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">association</a></span> · 联想</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-252">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-252" class="vocabulary-word-title">recollect<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-252" aria-label="定位 recollect">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-252" data-mark-label="recollect" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="recollect：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="recollect：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recollect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 recollect" title="在有道查询 recollect">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-252</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.记起，想起</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Old photographs remind me of places I can recall but whose names I cannot <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recollect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>recollect</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">老照片让我想起记得样貌却想不起名字的地方。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A familiar song can remind us of a journey and help us recall details we could not otherwise <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recollect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>recollect</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">熟悉歌曲能让我们想起旅程，并帮助记起原本想不起的细节。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The smell may remind you of childhood, helping you recall a kitchen and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=recollect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>recollect</strong></a> who cooked there.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">气味可能使你想起童年，帮助回忆厨房及在那里做饭的人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">recollect a name</span> · 想起名字</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=memory&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">memory</a></span> · 记忆</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=association&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">association</a></span> · 联想</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-253">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-253" class="vocabulary-word-title">remind<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-253" aria-label="定位 remind">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-253" data-mark-label="remind" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="remind：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="remind：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=remind&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 remind" title="在有道查询 remind">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-253</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.提醒，使想起</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Old photographs <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=remind&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>remind</strong></a> me of places I can recall but whose names I cannot recollect.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">老照片让我想起记得样貌却想不起名字的地方。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A familiar song can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=remind&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>remind</strong></a> us of a journey and help us recall details we could not otherwise recollect.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">熟悉歌曲能让我们想起旅程，并帮助记起原本想不起的细节。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The smell may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=remind&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>remind</strong></a> you of childhood, helping you recall a kitchen and recollect who cooked there.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">气味可能使你想起童年，帮助回忆厨房及在那里做饭的人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">remind someone of home</span> · 使人想起家</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=memory&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">memory</a></span> · 记忆</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=association&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">association</a></span> · 联想</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-254">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-254" class="vocabulary-word-title">retell<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-254" aria-label="定位 retell">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-254" data-mark-label="retell" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="retell：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="retell：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retell&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 retell" title="在有道查询 retell">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-254</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.重新讲述，复述</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In retrospect, I should <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retell&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>retell</strong></a> the story honestly rather than repeat an exaggeration to impress listeners.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">回想起来，我应诚实复述故事，而非为留下印象而重复夸张说法。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retell&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>retell</strong></a> a scene and repeat key lines to impress its rhythm on their memory, then discuss the performance in retrospect.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生复述场景并重复关键台词，将节奏印入记忆，再回顾表演。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In retrospect, attempts to impress the audience made him <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retell&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>retell</strong></a> the anecdote too often and repeat the same joke.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">回想起来，为给观众留下印象，他过于频繁地复述轶事、重复同一笑话。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">retell a story</span> · 复述故事</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=storytelling&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">storytelling</a></span> · 讲故事</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reflection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reflection</a></span> · 反思</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-255">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-255" class="vocabulary-word-title">repeat<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-255" aria-label="定位 repeat">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-255" data-mark-label="repeat" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="repeat：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="repeat：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=repeat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 repeat" title="在有道查询 repeat">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-255</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.重复；adj.再次光顾的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In retrospect, I should retell the story honestly rather than <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=repeat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>repeat</strong></a> an exaggeration to impress listeners.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">回想起来，我应诚实复述故事，而非为留下印象而重复夸张说法。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students retell a scene and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=repeat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>repeat</strong></a> key lines to impress its rhythm on their memory, then discuss the performance in retrospect.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生复述场景并重复关键台词，将节奏印入记忆，再回顾表演。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In retrospect, attempts to impress the audience made him retell the anecdote too often and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=repeat&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>repeat</strong></a> the same joke.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">回想起来，为给观众留下印象，他过于频繁地复述轶事、重复同一笑话。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">repeat a phrase</span> · 重复短语</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=storytelling&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">storytelling</a></span> · 讲故事</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reflection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reflection</a></span> · 反思</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-256">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-256" class="vocabulary-word-title">retrospect<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-256" aria-label="定位 retrospect">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-256" data-mark-label="retrospect" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="retrospect：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="retrospect：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retrospect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 retrospect" title="在有道查询 retrospect">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-256</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. [in－］回顾</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retrospect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>retrospect</strong></a>, I should retell the story honestly rather than repeat an exaggeration to impress listeners.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">回想起来，我应诚实复述故事，而非为留下印象而重复夸张说法。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students retell a scene and repeat key lines to impress its rhythm on their memory, then discuss the performance in <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retrospect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>retrospect</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生复述场景并重复关键台词，将节奏印入记忆，再回顾表演。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=retrospect&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>retrospect</strong></a>, attempts to impress the audience made him retell the anecdote too often and repeat the same joke.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">回想起来，为给观众留下印象，他过于频繁地复述轶事、重复同一笑话。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">in retrospect</span> · 回顾起来</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=storytelling&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">storytelling</a></span> · 讲故事</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reflection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reflection</a></span> · 反思</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-257">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-257" class="vocabulary-word-title">impress<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-257" aria-label="定位 impress">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-257" data-mark-label="impress" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="impress：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="impress：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=impress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 impress" title="在有道查询 impress">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-257</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.给......深刻印象；使铭记</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In retrospect, I should retell the story honestly rather than repeat an exaggeration to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=impress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>impress</strong></a> listeners.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">回想起来，我应诚实复述故事，而非为留下印象而重复夸张说法。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Students retell a scene and repeat key lines to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=impress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>impress</strong></a> its rhythm on their memory, then discuss the performance in retrospect.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">学生复述场景并重复关键台词，将节奏印入记忆，再回顾表演。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In retrospect, attempts to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=impress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>impress</strong></a> the audience made him retell the anecdote too often and repeat the same joke.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">回想起来，为给观众留下印象，他过于频繁地复述轶事、重复同一笑话。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">impress an audience</span> · 给观众留下印象</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=storytelling&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">storytelling</a></span> · 讲故事</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reflection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reflection</a></span> · 反思</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 20.26｜困难、窘迫与弥漫状态 {#group-20-26}

**串联方式：** 分别认克服困难、使羞愧与弥漫全境三种用法，依各自宾语建立搭配。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-258">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-258" class="vocabulary-word-title">surmount<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-258" aria-label="定位 surmount">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-258" data-mark-label="surmount" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="surmount：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="surmount：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=surmount&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 surmount" title="在有道查询 surmount">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-258</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.克服；置于......顶端</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A mistake may mortify a speaker, but confidence can pervade the room once she learns to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=surmount&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>surmount</strong></a> the setback.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">错误可能使演讲者窘迫，但当她学会克服挫折，信心便能弥漫全场。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Fear can pervade a workplace when minor errors mortify staff instead of helping them <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=surmount&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>surmount</strong></a> difficulties.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果小错误只会让员工羞窘，而非帮助其克服困难，恐惧就可能弥漫职场。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The mentor would never mortify a learner; she helped confidence pervade the class as students learned to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=surmount&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>surmount</strong></a> challenges.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">导师绝不会羞辱学习者；她帮助学生克服挑战，让信心充满课堂。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">surmount an obstacle</span> · 克服障碍</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=embarrassment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">embarrassment</a></span> · 窘迫</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=resilience&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">resilience</a></span> · 韧性</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-259">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-259" class="vocabulary-word-title">mortify<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-259" aria-label="定位 mortify">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-259" data-mark-label="mortify" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="mortify：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="mortify：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mortify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 mortify" title="在有道查询 mortify">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-259</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.使难堪，使羞愧</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A mistake may <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mortify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mortify</strong></a> a speaker, but confidence can pervade the room once she learns to surmount the setback.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">错误可能使演讲者窘迫，但当她学会克服挫折，信心便能弥漫全场。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Fear can pervade a workplace when minor errors <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mortify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mortify</strong></a> staff instead of helping them surmount difficulties.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果小错误只会让员工羞窘，而非帮助其克服困难，恐惧就可能弥漫职场。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The mentor would never <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mortify&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mortify</strong></a> a learner; she helped confidence pervade the class as students learned to surmount challenges.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">导师绝不会羞辱学习者；她帮助学生克服挑战，让信心充满课堂。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">mortify a speaker</span> · 使发言者窘迫</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=embarrassment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">embarrassment</a></span> · 窘迫</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=resilience&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">resilience</a></span> · 韧性</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-260">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-260" class="vocabulary-word-title">pervade<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-260" aria-label="定位 pervade">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-260" data-mark-label="pervade" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="pervade：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="pervade：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pervade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 pervade" title="在有道查询 pervade">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-260</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.弥漫，渗透，遍及</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A mistake may mortify a speaker, but confidence can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pervade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pervade</strong></a> the room once she learns to surmount the setback.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">错误可能使演讲者窘迫，但当她学会克服挫折，信心便能弥漫全场。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Fear can <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pervade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pervade</strong></a> a workplace when minor errors mortify staff instead of helping them surmount difficulties.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果小错误只会让员工羞窘，而非帮助其克服困难，恐惧就可能弥漫职场。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The mentor would never mortify a learner; she helped confidence <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pervade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pervade</strong></a> the class as students learned to surmount challenges.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">导师绝不会羞辱学习者；她帮助学生克服挑战，让信心充满课堂。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">pervade the room</span> · 弥漫房间</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=embarrassment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">embarrassment</a></span> · 窘迫</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=resilience&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">resilience</a></span> · 韧性</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 20.27｜替代与区分 {#group-20-27}

**串联方式：** 先练 replace 和 substitute 的不同结构，再练 distinguish 和 differentiate。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-261">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-261" class="vocabulary-word-title">replace<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-261" aria-label="定位 replace">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-261" data-mark-label="replace" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="replace：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="replace：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=replace&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 replace" title="在有道查询 replace">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-261</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">V.代替，取代</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=replace&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>replace</strong></a> a component or substitute a material, distinguish essential features and differentiate the available options.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">更换零件或替代材料前，应辨明核心特征并区分可选方案。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Teachers must distinguish learning needs, differentiate tasks and decide whether to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=replace&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>replace</strong></a> a text or substitute an easier exercise.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教师须分辨学习需求、区分任务，并决定更换课文还是代以更简单练习。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Labels help consumers distinguish products, differentiate brands and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=replace&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>replace</strong></a> an item only when they find a suitable substitute.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">标签帮助消费者分辨产品、区分品牌，并只在找到合适替代品时更换物品。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">replace a component</span> · 更换零件</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=comparison&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">comparison</a></span> · 比较</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=alternative&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">alternative</a></span> · 替代选择</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-262">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-262" class="vocabulary-word-title">substitute<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-262" aria-label="定位 substitute">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-262" data-mark-label="substitute" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="substitute：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="substitute：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=substitute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 substitute" title="在有道查询 substitute">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-262</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.替换；n.代用品；代替者；替补(运动员)</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you replace a component or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=substitute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>substitute</strong></a> a material, distinguish essential features and differentiate the available options.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">更换零件或替代材料前，应辨明核心特征并区分可选方案。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Teachers must distinguish learning needs, differentiate tasks and decide whether to replace a text or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=substitute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>substitute</strong></a> an easier exercise.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教师须分辨学习需求、区分任务，并决定更换课文还是代以更简单练习。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Labels help consumers distinguish products, differentiate brands and replace an item only when they find a suitable <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=substitute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>substitute</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">标签帮助消费者分辨产品、区分品牌，并只在找到合适替代品时更换物品。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">substitute one material for another</span> · 用一种材料替代另一种</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=comparison&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">comparison</a></span> · 比较</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=alternative&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">alternative</a></span> · 替代选择</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="20-263">
<summary><h4 id="word-20-263" class="vocabulary-word-title">distinguish<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-20-263" aria-label="定位 distinguish">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="20-263" data-mark-label="distinguish" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="distinguish：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="distinguish：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=distinguish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 distinguish" title="在有道查询 distinguish">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">20-263</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.区别，辨别；使出众，使著名</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you replace a component or substitute a material, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=distinguish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>distinguish</strong></a> essential features and differentiate the available options.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">更换零件或替代材料前，应辨明核心特征并区分可选方案。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Teachers must <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=distinguish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>distinguish</strong></a> learning needs, differentiate tasks and decide whether to replace a text or substitute an easier exercise.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教师须分辨学习需求、区分任务，并决定更换课文还是代以更简单练习。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Labels help consumers <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=distinguish&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>distinguish</strong></a> products, differentiate brands and replace an item only when they find a suitable substitute.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">标签帮助消费者分辨产品、区分品牌，并只在找到合适替代品时更换物品。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">distinguish fact from opinion</span> · 区分事实与观点</p>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you replace a component or substitute a material, distinguish essential features and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=differentiate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>differentiate</strong></a> the available options.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">更换零件或替代材料前，应辨明核心特征并区分可选方案。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Teachers must distinguish learning needs, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=differentiate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>differentiate</strong></a> tasks and decide whether to replace a text or substitute an easier exercise.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教师须分辨学习需求、区分任务，并决定更换课文还是代以更简单练习。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Labels help consumers distinguish products, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=differentiate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>differentiate</strong></a> brands and replace an item only when they find a suitable substitute.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">标签帮助消费者分辨产品、区分品牌，并只在找到合适替代品时更换物品。</p></li>
</ol>

### 词群 20.28｜倾斜与方向 {#group-20-28}

**串联方式：** 用倾斜、倚靠和侧向移动组织一个身体或物体姿态场景。

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If you lean sideways as the road begins to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=incline&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>incline</strong></a>, you may lose your balance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">道路开始倾斜时，如果你向侧面倾身，可能失去平衡。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The instructor asked us to lean sideways and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=incline&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>incline</strong></a> our heads gently.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">教练让我们向侧边倾身，并轻轻低头。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The roof will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=incline&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>incline</strong></a> toward the garden, while supports lean sideways to match the design.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">屋顶将向花园倾斜，支柱则按设计侧向倾斜。</p></li>
</ol>

</details>

### 本章 Language Takeaway

优先尝试主动说出的三个表达：

- **respond to a question**：回应问题；question 为补充词，可与 query、questionnaire 联系辨认。
- **cope with a problem**：应对问题。
- **replace A with B**：用 B 替换 A。

### 串学示范｜自拟短文

When we face a problem, we first observe what has happened and explain the situation. We may assign tasks, seek information and verify a claim. If an attempt fails, we can adjust our approach, restore what was damaged and try again. Clear actions make a description more precise.

**中文理解：** 面对问题时，我们先观察发生了什么并解释情况，随后分配任务、寻找信息和核验说法。如果尝试失败，可以调整方法、恢复受损部分并再次尝试。明确的动作能让描述更精确。

### 闭卷回忆｜先尝试，再逐级提示

**任务：** 选一个生活问题，用明确动作说明你怎样观察、沟通、处理和修复。

1. 关掉本章正文，在空白页列出你记得的词群；每群先写 3—5 个英文词或搭配。
2. 从中选 3 个词群，连成 3—5 句英文；用明确关系连接，专业分类和事实不确定时先核对。
3. 卡住时，先只看“本章主线”；还想不起，再看词群标题和中文简释，最后才看英文。
4. 用核对编号找出遗漏词；把“见到认得”与“想表达时能说出”分开记录。
5. 合上提示重新说一遍；下次换一个对象、地点或例子，检查能否迁移。

可直接复制的空白页：

```text
主题：行为动作
我记得的词群：
每群想起的词或搭配：
今天的 3 个 takeaway：
我的 3—5 句英文：
看提示后才想起的核对编号：
下次换什么情境再说：
```
